# Digital Payments and Fintech: progress report, 2025-09-01 to 2026-09-06

*54 countries. Each row below is carried verbatim from that country's own progress report, which answers a fixed frame of indicators over the period; nothing is written here. A country not listed under an indicator has ***No evidence*** on it in its own report.*

**Progress values.** *Advanced* — a system entered service, a stage was completed or an instrument was made. *Stalled* — a stated target passed without delivery. *Regressed* — an instrument was withdrawn or neutralised, or a reported position worsened. *Mixed* — the indicator's instruments moved in different directions in the period; the clause after the comma names which moved which way. *No change* — the base holds a standing position and nothing in the period touched it. ***No evidence*** — the base holds nothing on this indicator at all. A value may carry a qualifying clause after a comma, as in *Advanced, regulations still pending*.

*The place reports do not share one window; the period above is the range they span.*

## Governance role of central bank

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | A [national payments committee was given its composition and working arrangements by regulation on 31 May 2026](https://www.echoroukonline.com/%d8%aa%d8%ad%d8%b1%d9%83-%d8%b1%d8%b3%d9%85%d9%8a-%d9%84%d8%a5%d9%86%d9%87%d8%a7%d8%a1-%d9%87%d9%8a%d9%85%d9%86%d8%a9-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%83%d8%a7%d8%b4-%d9%88%d8%aa%d9%82%d8%b1%d9%8a%d8%b1-%d8%b3). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The committee was created by the 2023 monetary and banking law with no operating framework published. A [regulation of 31 May 2026 set its composition and working arrangements: an advisory body seated at the central bank, chaired by the Governor, seating seven ministries, the postal operator, the interbank card and electronic-money bodies, the banking association, two appointed experts and the internal security, gendarmerie and police directorates, owing government a published annual report on payment instruments and periodic statistics](https://www.echoroukonline.com/%d8%aa%d8%ad%d8%b1%d9%83-%d8%b1%d8%b3%d9%85%d9%8a-%d9%84%d8%a5%d9%86%d9%87%d8%a7%d8%a1-%d9%87%d9%8a%d9%85%d9%86%d8%a9-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%83%d8%a7%d8%b4-%d9%88%d8%aa%d9%82%d8%b1%d9%8a%d8%b1-%d8%b3). The national payments strategy is not drafted and no annual report is published; the regulation text is not held.<br><br>[Digital-bank licensing was established and a binding know-your-customer instruction issued on 30 April 2026](https://www.elmoudjahid.dz/fr/actualite/dispositif-kyc-la-banque-d-algerie-prepare-une-nouvelle-instruction-251312), [binding on banks for anti-money-laundering and politically-exposed-person scrutiny](https://www.echoroukonline.com/%D8%A7%D8%B9%D8%B1%D9%81-%D8%B2%D8%A8%D9%88%D9%86%D9%83-%D8%A8%D8%AF%D8%A7%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%B9%D8%B5%D8%B1-%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF-%D9%85%D9%86-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AF%D9%82%D9%8A%D9%82-%D8%A7). A [digital currency is argued for in the press with no central-bank instrument, pilot, design or timetable on file](https://www.echoroukonline.com/%D8%AA%D9%88%D8%B8%D9%8A%D9%81-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B0%D9%83%D8%A7%D8%A1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%B5%D8%B7%D9%86%D8%A7%D8%B9%D9%8A-%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A8%D9%84%D9%88%D9%83-%D8%AA%D8%B4%D9%8A%D9%86).</details> | Advanced |
| Angola | The central bank [completed migration of domestic payments to the international messaging standard on 10 July 2026](https://angop.ao/noticias/economia/bna-conclui-migracao-para-padrao-internacional-iso-20022/), across the switch, exchange and settlement participants. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-16 — the central bank [announced completion of the migration of domestic payments to the ISO 20022 messaging standard on 10 July 2026, across the switch, the exchange and the real-time settlement participants](https://angop.ao/noticias/economia/bna-conclui-migracao-para-padrao-internacional-iso-20022/).<br><br>Only the completion is dated. No programme start, scope document or cost is held, and no participant count, message volume or exception rate is published, so the base records that the migration finished and nothing about what it changed.<br><br>It is the one act of payments-system governance on this ledger for the window. The instant payment scheme and the regional settlement arrangement reported below both run under this bank, and neither carries a supervisory statement, a scheme rulebook or an oversight report on the base.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | The regional central bank digital currency [is in pilot](https://lanation.bj/economie/crypto-actifs-le-debat-sur-les-opportunites-et-les-risques-ouvert), against a domestic debate on crypto-asset opportunity and risk. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-04-29 — [the regional electronic currency is in pilot, alongside a domestic debate on crypto-asset opportunity and risk](https://lanation.bj/economie/crypto-actifs-le-debat-sur-les-opportunites-et-les-risques-ouvert). No baseline preceded it on this ledger.<br><br>Monetary and payments governance for Benin is regional rather than national: the instant payment rail, the currency pilot and the union-wide account statistics all sit with the regional central bank, and no domestic instrument, supervisory statement or scheme rule appears on this ledger. The one national payments intervention in the window came from a consumer-redress body, reported under consumer protection.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | A national payment switch [is funded in plan only, inside a P486m allocation covering four projects together](https://www.sundaystandard.info/finance-ministry-bets-p8-8bn-on-digital-discipline/), with no build, procurement, operator or date. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-10-31 — [a national payment switch is named inside a P486m ICT allocation sought under the twelfth development plan](https://www.sundaystandard.info/finance-ministry-bets-p8-8bn-on-digital-discipline/), [part of a P8.8bn digital ask](https://www.sundaystandard.info/finance-ministry-bets-p8-8bn-on-digital-discipline/). The allocation covers the switch, an accounting-system upgrade, a sandbox and electronic procurement together, and the switch carries no separate figure. No build, procurement, operator or date is on the record.<br><br>So domestic interbank routing has no national infrastructure and no dated plan, while the cross-border corridor reported below is live and the payments regulations were tightened in April. The central bank's visible action in the window is regulatory rather than infrastructural.</details> | No change |
| Burundi | The central bank [migrated the payment switch to the international messaging standard](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-upgrades-payment-system-to-international-iso-20022-standard/) and [issued five anti-money-laundering circulars](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-modernizes-financial-crime-defense-as-central-bank-issues-new-aml-cft-rules/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-02-05 — [the payment switch was migrated to the ISO 20022 messaging standard](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-upgrades-payment-system-to-international-iso-20022-standard/), from a position of not being migrated. It is the technical precondition for the instant payment system launched two months later and for the regional accessions reported under cross-border functionality.<br><br>2026-02-16 — [five central bank circulars on anti-money-laundering and counter-terrorist financing were issued, aligned to the international standard-setter's recommendations](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-modernizes-financial-crime-defense-as-central-bank-issues-new-aml-cft-rules/), where no baseline was held.<br><br>Both are the bank acting as scheme owner rather than as supervisor: no oversight report, scheme rulebook or published sanction appears on this ledger, and the mandatory-integration penalty reported below is the only enforcement instrument on record.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | A [tariff-grid revision went to consultation at Douala on 30 July 2026](https://leconomie.info/article/paiements-numeriques-gimacpay-enregistre-203-millions-de-transactions-pour-863-milliards-de-fcfa-en-2025), the operator conceding that pricing penalises low-value transactions. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-30 to 31 - a consultation workshop opened and closed on revising the regional switch's tariff grid, [the operator conceding that current pricing penalises low-value transactions and drives artificial splitting of payments](https://leconomie.info/article/paiements-numeriques-gimacpay-enregistre-203-millions-de-transactions-pour-863-milliards-de-fcfa-en-2025).<br><br>No revised grid, effective date or fee schedule has been published. Pricing that penalises small payments is the mechanism by which an interoperable rail stays a large-ticket rail, which the merchant-payment figures elsewhere in this report show it currently is; the consultation is the first acknowledgement of it on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Cape Verde | The central bank [announced SPEED, a national instant-transfer system, for launch in the first quarter of 2026](https://vozdoarchipelago.cv/noticias/bcv-anuncia-spid-o-novo-sistema-de-transferencias-instantaneas/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The governor of the Banco de Cabo Verde announced SPEED on 5 December 2025, a national immediate-transfer system to launch in the first quarter of 2026, following a pilot completed with four commercial banks and specified to work without internet access](https://vozdoarchipelago.cv/noticias/bcv-anuncia-spid-o-novo-sistema-de-transferencias-instantaneas/). The bank is acting as architect and coordinator of the infrastructure rather than as its overseer, with costs shared between it, the commercial banks, the regulator and the government.<br><br>[SICIL, the integrated interbank clearing and settlement system, is separately described in service by the bank](https://www.bcv.cv/en/SistemadePagamentos/CompensacaoInterbancaria/Paginas/CompensacaoInterbancariaeLiquidacaoSICIL.aspx).<br><br>No launch confirmation for SPEED is held, and the base carries nothing on the digital currency the bank said it was separately studying.</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | The central bank's [interbank settlement system entered service on 23 April 2026](https://banque-comores.km/article/show/ceremonie-de-lancement-des-nouveaux-moyens-de-paiement), cutting cheque settlement to one working day. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system [entered service on 23 April 2026 with a presidential launch on 4 May, operating over a dedicated 120 km fibre network, with an automated clearing leg under KMF 10,000,000 and a real-time gross settlement leg above it, cheque settlement falling from two or three days to one working day](https://banque-comores.km/uploads/BCC-livret%283%29.pdf). It is interbank only, with no retail leg, and was [launched alongside two other systems](https://alwatwan.net/economie/secteur-bancaire-i-les-comores-inaugurent-de-nouveaux-modes-et-syst%C3%A8mes-de-paiement.html).<br><br>Volumes are [more than 150 transfers a day worth about KMF 125,000,000 and 850 cheques a day worth about KMF 1,000,000,000 on the clearing leg, and about forty operations a day for KMF 3,700,000,000 on the gross settlement leg](https://banque-comores.km/uploads/BCC-livret%283%29.pdf), operator-stated and published without an as-of date.<br><br>A [government securities market has regulations in force, actors trained and pilot tests passed but first operations not launched, after eight monetary-fund missions over three years](https://www.mayottehebdo.com/actualite/economie/comores-lancement-de-nouveaux-systemes-de-paiement/).</details> | Advanced |
| Congo | The [treasury single account at the regional central bank was still being finalised on the day the payment platform went live](https://leconomie.info/congo-le-paiement-des-impots-et-taxes-bascule-en-ligne/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-03 - the finance ministry [stated the opening convention was still being finalised on the day the payment platform went live, so the single account intended to centralise the receipts the platform routes is not in place](https://leconomie.info/congo-le-paiement-des-impots-et-taxes-bascule-en-ligne/).<br><br>The base holds no earlier position on the account, so the direction is withheld. The [related special treasury account is voted FCFA 5,968,000,000 for 2026](https://sgg.cg/JO/2026/congo-jo-2026-3-3.pdf), more than double the prior year, which is the only quantified signal on the treasury's banking function held.</details> | No change, a first statement of the account's position with nothing earlier behind it |
| Cote d'Ivoire | A [fourth electronic-money licence was granted in September 2025](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2637099/economie/inclusion-financieremonnaie-electronique-la-cote-divoire-enregistre-un-4e-etablissement), and the central bank made instant-payment interoperability compulsory. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-09 - a [fourth electronic-money institution was licensed, issuing a locally built wallet](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2637099/economie/inclusion-financieremonnaie-electronique-la-cote-divoire-enregistre-un-4e-etablissement), against three at the window's opening. A later account names a fifth operator among electronic-money institutions, which this count does not include.<br><br>2026-04-02 - a [central bank communique made instant-payment interoperability compulsory from 30 June 2026 for banks, microfinance institutions, electronic-money institutions and payment institutions](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2641755/economie/uemoa-linteroperabilite-des-paiements-devient-obligatoire-des-le-30-juin-2026).<br><br>Supervision is exercised regionally and reported regionally: the only market data held is the [union-wide annual report on digital financial services](https://www.bceao.int/sites/default/files/2026-03/Rapport%20annuel%20sur%20les%20services%20financiers%20num%C3%A9riques%20dans%20l%27UEMOA%20-%202024.pdf), and no Ivorian supervisory finding, sanction or licence withdrawal is on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | The [central bank licenses and supervises every payment service by law](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-n118-an-15-7eme-l-portant-creation-dun-systeme-de-paiement-national-sa-reglementation-et-sa-surveillance/) and [operates the settlement system itself](https://banque-centrale.dj/infrastructures-ats/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The 2016 law [applies to every payment service and system operated in the country and empowers the central bank to license, regulate and supervise payment-system providers, defining electronic money and electronic funds transfer](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-n118-an-15-7eme-l-portant-creation-dun-systeme-de-paiement-national-sa-reglementation-et-sa-surveillance/).<br><br>The bank is also the operator: the [settlement platform combining real-time gross settlement and automated clearing has run since 15 July 2021, with the Treasury among its participants](https://banque-centrale.dj/infrastructures-ats/).<br><br>It acts by instruction rather than by statute - from [31 August 2025 non-conforming cheques were refused by the platform and legacy stock withdrawn](https://banque-centrale.dj/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/BCD-Communique-SYRAD.pdf). No supervisory decision, licence register or sanction is published.</details> | No change |
| DR Congo | The central bank [set 9 April 2027 as the date from which no foreign-cash transaction of any amount is permitted](https://actualite.cd/2026/05/04/inclusion-financiere-en-rdc-58-dacces-contre-25-30-de-bancarisation-la-bcc-parie-sur-la). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-05 - the central bank [set 9 April 2027 as the date from which no foreign-cash transaction of any amount is permitted](https://actualite.cd/2026/05/04/inclusion-financiere-en-rdc-58-dacces-contre-25-30-de-bancarisation-la-bcc-parie-sur-la). The deadline stands with no implementing instrument, exemption schedule or enforcement mechanism held.<br><br>It runs directly against the dollar-denominated rails proliferating around it, including a [stablecoin settlement use case live on a card-network partner's rail](https://techafricanews.com/2026/04/16/new-visa-and-bcc-agreement-targets-financial-inclusion-and-payment-modernization-in-congo/) whose peg is inferred rather than disclosed. No held source reconciles the two, and no supervisory statement addresses what a de-dollarised retail payment system would settle in.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | The central bank gave [initial approval to a first wholly digital bank](https://itweb.africa/article/central-bank-of-egypt-approves-first-digital-bank/Gb3Bw7WaApPq2k6V) and [preliminary approval to a second](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/21/cib-secures-preliminary-approval-to-launch-yomo-digital-bank-in-egypt/); neither is operating. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The first had [preliminary clearance from May 2024 with due diligence on infrastructure, banking systems and cybersecurity under way](https://itweb.africa/article/central-bank-of-egypt-approves-first-digital-bank/Gb3Bw7WaApPq2k6V); initial approval was granted with products stated as preparing for 2026 and no launch on record, so nothing held establishes an operating digital bank in the country.<br><br>2026-08-21 - a commercial bank received [preliminary approval to establish a digitally native bank under the 2023 digital-banks framework, now entering technology validation, cybersecurity testing and operational-resilience readiness under a newly appointed chief executive, launch remaining subject to final approval, with no launch date or capitalisation stated](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/21/cib-secures-preliminary-approval-to-launch-yomo-digital-bank-in-egypt/).<br><br>Two approvals and no operating institution is the position after two years of the framework.</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | [No mobile-money licensing decision is on record](https://thefintechtimes.com/equatorial-guineas-fintech-ecosystem-in-2026/), and bank and telecom applications have launched without one being reported. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The absence of a licensed operator was named by the World Bank diagnostic and nothing has changed it ([diagnostic](https://www.worldbank.org/en/country/equatorialguinea/publication/equatorial-guinea-digital-economy-country-diagnostic-bridging-the-gaps-to-develop-a-safe-and-inclusive-digital-transform)).<br><br>Two wallets nonetheless entered service during 2026 ([market account](https://thefintechtimes.com/equatorial-guineas-fintech-ecosystem-in-2026/)), so services are running ahead of the authorisation that would govern them, which is the same pattern as the data-protection statute without its supervisory body.</details> | No change |
| Eritrea | The Bank of Eritrea [gave holders of cash outside the banking system until 31 July 2026 to deposit it](https://awate.com/deposit-your-money-or-else-eritreas-banking-ultimatum/), on pain of legal action. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-02 — a legal notice from the Bank of Eritrea [ordered cash held outside banks deposited by 31 July 2026](https://awate.com/deposit-your-money-or-else-eritreas-banking-ultimatum/), with legal action threatened against holders who do not comply. The notice defines no threshold for the excessive holding it names and states no enforcement mechanism.<br><br>It is the only central-bank action on this ledger, and it addresses the cash economy rather than any payment system: no named mobile-money or digital-payments service appears anywhere on the base for Eritrea.</details> | Advanced, an instrument aimed at the cash economy rather than at payment |
| Eswatini | A [National FinTech Strategy 2025-2030](https://www.times.co.sz/business/readmore.php?bhsadjgfoh=FinTech+Strategy+to+transform+Eswatini%E2%80%99s+digital+future&bvhdgsj=Business+and+Economy&yiphi=2100) was launched by the central bank governor and the finance minister, above a [digital currency design paper](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/wp-content/uploads/2021/04/2024-Fintech-Digital-Lilangeni-Design-Paper.pdf) published in 2024. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The strategy was launched in December 2025, framed around reducing reliance on cash ([strategy](https://www.times.co.sz/business/readmore.php?bhsadjgfoh=FinTech+Strategy+to+transform+Eswatini%E2%80%99s+digital+future&bvhdgsj=Business+and+Economy&yiphi=2100), [cash](https://www.times.co.sz/business/readmore.php?bhsadjgfoh=FinTech+to+reduce+cash+reliance&bvhdgsj=Business+and+Economy&yiphi=2077)).<br><br>The design paper is not an issuance decision, and no pilot, launch date or governance arrangement follows it ([paper](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/wp-content/uploads/2021/04/2024-Fintech-Digital-Lilangeni-Design-Paper.pdf)).<br><br>The central bank's annual integrated reports are held for two successive years, the later superseding a payment-switch participation series the base had carried from earlier reporting ([report](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Annual-Integrated-Report-2025-2026-online-compress.pdf), [prior](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/wp-content/uploads/2021/04/Annual-Integrated-Report-2024-25.pdf)).<br><br>The settlement layer under all of it is old and undocumented since: the interbank payment and settlement system runs under a rulebook adopted by its steering committee in May 2007 ([rulebook](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/wp-content/uploads/2021/03/SWIPSS-Rules-Procedures.pdf)), and the central bank describes its role as facilitating development and overseeing the safety and soundness of national payment systems ([mandate](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/national-payment-systems/)). No revision of the rulebook, and no volume or value statistic for the system, is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | The central bank published a [five-year payments strategy](https://www.ena.et/web/eng/ethiopia-ndps-2026-2030) in December 2025 and directed the banking system to harmonise accounts with the national identity. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The strategy for 2026 to 2030 was published against 18.5tn birr a year in digital transactions ([payments strategy](https://www.ena.et/web/eng/ethiopia-ndps-2026-2030)).<br><br>The following day the bank set a March 2026 deadline for full integration of the national identity with the banking system ([banking integration](https://www.nbe.gov.et/fayda-banking-integration)).<br><br>The bank's supervisory record is thinner than its strategy: no licensing register, supervisory report or consumer-complaint statistics are held.</details> | Advanced |
| Gabon | A [regional interoperable QR standard](https://www.gabonreview.com/cemac-moins-de-cash-plus-de-numerique-la-beac-deploie-le-qr-code-interoperable/) took effect under an April 2026 regulation, routed through the regional switch with the Gabonese scheme as its pilot. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The standard was launched at Douala under a regional monetary union regulation of 8 April 2026 and is in force across the six member states, settled through the regional switch ([standard](https://leconomie.info/article/paiement-numerique-le-gimac-officialise-la-norme-qr-code-interoperable-de-la-cemac)).<br><br>Merchant-presented mode is the primary mode, explicitly to avoid the capital cost of terminals ([launch](https://www.gabonreview.com/cemac-moins-de-cash-plus-de-numerique-la-beac-deploie-le-qr-code-interoperable/)).<br><br>The national scheme is the pilot for it, which is the base's only evidence of the central bank shaping domestic payments rather than the region setting the rule.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | A [payment systems advisory committee](https://www.gamswitch.com/press-release/the-gambia-launches-national-payment-systems-advisory-committee-npsac-to-strengthen-payment-system-governance) was launched under central bank chairmanship in February 2026 for a three-year term. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The bank's standing instruments are old: a [mobile money regulation](https://www.cbg.gm/downloads-file/b2064e61-f37b-11e9-876c-02e599c15748) of 2011 carrying an interoperability duty, and a [cost-recovery pricing policy](https://www.cbg.gm/downloads-file/448bc45a-06e7-11ea-876c-02e599c15748) effective December 2013.<br><br>Governance has thickened in the window: the committee was [launched on 23 February 2026 for three years](https://www.gamswitch.com/press-release/the-gambia-launches-national-payment-systems-advisory-committee-npsac-to-strengthen-payment-system-governance), and the monetary policy committee [ordered deposit reserve rules and complaint tracking for fintechs](https://www.cbg.gm/downloads-file/c76e08c3-12ec-11f1-8725-02e599c15748) in December 2025. Both records reach the base through the switch operator or the minutes rather than a primary supervisory publication.</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | The central bank [revoked an electronic money issuer's licence](https://www.myjoyonline.com/bog-officials-police-restrict-access-to-zeepay-headquarters-after-licence-revocation/) in July 2026 and opened enforcement against unlicensed digital credit apps. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The licence was revoked on 14 July 2026 for electronic money not fully cash-backed, with premises access restricted by the bank and police ([revocation](https://www.myjoyonline.com/bog-officials-police-restrict-access-to-zeepay-headquarters-after-licence-revocation/)).<br><br>A September 2025 directive on digital credit service providers took effect from 1 November 2025 with applications from that month; the 30 June 2026 deadline elapsed and twenty unlicensed apps were named on 3 August 2026, the notice that set the deadline not held ([enforcement](https://www.bog.gov.gh/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Notice-No.25-BOG-SEC-GOV-2026-Notice-PUBLICATION-OF-UNLICENSED-DIGITAL-CREDIT-SERVICE-PROVIDERS-ON-THE-BANKS-WEBSITE-SOCIAL-MEDIA-PLATFORMS-AND-OTHER-COMM-CHANNELS-1.pdf), [earlier notice](https://www.bog.gov.gh/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Notice-No.22-BOG-SEC-GOV-2026-UNLICENSED-DIGITAL-CREDIT-SERVICES-PROVIDERS-1.pdf)).<br><br>Structural separation of the dominant operator's mobile-money subsidiary completed in the first quarter of 2026, the subsidiary filing its own condensed statements inside the parent's report and declaring its own interim dividend, its statutory basis stated only by the secondary account ([separation](https://techcabal.com/2026/04/03/mtn-completes-ghana-momo-standalone-fintech-arm/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea | [NimbaPay launched on 22 July 2026 as the national instant and interoperable payment platform](https://guineenews.org/2026/07/22/guinee-la-bcrg-lance-nimbapay-la-plateforme-nationale-qui-revolutionne-les-paiements-instantanes-et-linteroperabilite/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[NimbaPay was officially launched on 22 July 2026 under the Prime Minister's patronage, operated by the Guineenne de la Monetique under the central bank, letting any user send and receive money instantly between a bank, a microfinance institution and an electronic-money establishment](https://guineenews.org/2026/07/22/guinee-la-bcrg-lance-nimbapay-la-plateforme-nationale-qui-revolutionne-les-paiements-instantanes-et-linteroperabilite/). [The governor said fragmented systems had been penalising inter-institutional transfers, and named digitisation of state payments and cross-border flows as the next steps](https://guineenews.org/2026/07/22/guinee-la-bcrg-lance-nimbapay-la-plateforme-nationale-qui-revolutionne-les-paiements-instantanes-et-linteroperabilite/).<br><br>No transaction volume or participant count is held for the platform since launch.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | The [central bank register](https://www.bceao.int/sites/default/files/inline-files/4%20-%20Liste%20Guin%C3%A9e-Bissau_2026.pdf) carries six banks, one payment institution and one electronic money issuer as at June 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Payment governance is the monetary union's rather than the country's. The [first licensed payment institution in Guinea-Bissau appeared on the union register in September 2025](https://www.bceao.int/sites/default/files/2025-09/LISTE_DES_ETABLISSEMENTS_DE_PAIEMENT_AGREES_15_septembre_2025.pdf), and the [June 2026 register lists six banks, one payment institution and one electronic money issuer](https://www.bceao.int/sites/default/files/inline-files/4%20-%20Liste%20Guin%C3%A9e-Bissau_2026.pdf).<br><br>No domestic payments regulator, national switch governance or oversight publication appears in the base.</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | The central bank is [designing a fast payment system and national switch](https://techcabal.com/2026/04/03/kenswitch-visa-deal-stakes-kenya-payments-overhaul/) amid heavy lobbying, with no award. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Three contenders are named and no design document, timetable or award is on file ([contest](https://techcabal.com/2026/04/03/kenswitch-visa-deal-stakes-kenya-payments-overhaul/)).<br><br>The bank's other principal act in the window was to set mobile-money pricing principles that operate through voluntary operator alignment, so on both the switch and the tariff the regulator's position rests on negotiation rather than on an instrument.</details> | No change |
| Lesotho | The central bank's [national payment switch](https://publiceyenews.com/2025/05/20/leswitch-expands-to-pos-transactions) has localised instant payments, ATM and point-of-sale transactions across four commercial banks. <details><summary>Full record</summary>LeSwitch carries instant payments from 2022, ATM localisation from October 2024 and point-of-sale localisation from 11 May 2025, across four commercial banks ([point of sale](https://lestimes.com/?p=87043), [central bank](https://publiceyenews.com/2025/05/20/leswitch-expands-to-pos-transactions), [switch](https://www.standardlesothobank.co.ls/lesotho/personal/About-us/press-releases/press-release-lesotho-national-payment-switch-leswitch)).<br><br>E-commerce and retail were named as the next steps and nothing later is held, so the switch's coverage is recorded as at May 2025.</details> | No change |
| Liberia | A [directive of September 2025](https://cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/NEPS-IIPS%20Directive%20-%20092325.pdf) routes all government mobile-money payments and collections through the national switch. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[Directive CBL/E-GOV/DIR/002/2025](https://cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/NEPS-IIPS%20Directive%20-%20092325.pdf) requires every government payment to a beneficiary by mobile money — salaries, pensions, social benefits and other transfers — to route exclusively through the national switch from 27 October 2025, and all revenue collections by mobile money from 1 December 2025.<br><br>The bank's [2025 annual report](https://www.cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/CENTRAL%20BANK%20OF%20LIBERIA%20ANNUAL%20REPORT%202025.pdf) sets out the rest of what it governs: the payment system section, the regional settlement system, the instant payment system and mobile money, with a first series of cross-border transfers terminating into mobile wallets. Its [2026 policy statement](https://cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/CENTRAL%20BANK%20OF%20LIBERIA%20%202026%20POLICY%20STATEMENT.pdf) adds continuity and resilience testing obligations for participants.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | The central bank launched a [national financial inclusion strategy for 2025-2029](https://cbl.gov.ly/en/governor-of-the-central-bank-of-libya-announces-from-tripoli-the-launch-of-the-national-financial-inclusion-strategy-and-a-number-of-transformative-projects-during-the-opening-of-the-electronic-paymen/), directed banks to activate [QR acceptance](https://alwasat.ly/news/libya/510123) and launched a [stand-in processing service](https://libyaherald.com/2026/06/central-bank-of-libya-governor-inaugurates-2nd-libya-international-electronic-payment-exhibition-epaix-2026). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The governor launched a [national financial inclusion strategy for 2025-2029 at the first electronic payment forum in June 2025, alongside financial-inclusion accounts for excluded groups, a banking data protection system, full digitisation of government payments, localisation of the foreign-currency payments infrastructure, a framework for digital and crypto currencies and a digital banking identity](https://cbl.gov.ly/en/governor-of-the-central-bank-of-libya-announces-from-tripoli-the-launch-of-the-national-financial-inclusion-strategy-and-a-number-of-transformative-projects-during-the-opening-of-the-electronic-paymen/). The strategy text is not published and none of the projects carries a budget, operator or delivery date.<br><br>Banks were directed to activate QR-code acceptance by 8 March 2026 ([directive](https://alwasat.ly/news/libya/510123)).<br><br>Circular 9 of 2026, issued on 11 March, permitted resident foreigners to hold e-wallets on passport and residency with daily transfer caps ([circular](https://alwasat.ly/news/libya/511044)).<br><br>A national stand-in processing service was launched at the EPAIX conference on 17 June 2026 to keep card payments working through communications and banking disruption ([service](https://libyaherald.com/2026/06/central-bank-of-libya-governor-inaugurates-2nd-libya-international-electronic-payment-exhibition-epaix-2026)). The bank sets the rules, the deadlines and the shared infrastructure, and no other body is recorded acting on the payment market.</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | A [vendor was selected](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/ecurrency-cbdc-technology-in-madagascar-for-eariary-project-at-banky-foibeni-madagasikara-bfm-302597601.html) in October 2025 for the central bank digital currency pilot, with the bank sole issuer and distribution private; no live pilot is reported. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Testing had been announced in May 2025 with no vendor named ([testing](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/finance/madagascar-tests-e-ariary-its-central-bank-digital-currency-to-modernize-payments)).<br><br>A platform was selected in October 2025 on a model where the central bank is sole issuer and distribution is private, and no live pilot has been reported since ([selection](https://www.prnewswire.com/news-releases/ecurrency-cbdc-technology-in-madagascar-for-eariary-project-at-banky-foibeni-madagasikara-bfm-302597601.html)). No other supervisory act, licensing decision or payment-system rule of the central bank is held in this base.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | A [National Payments Systems Vision and Strategy 2026-2030](https://itweb.africa/article/malawi-unveils-digital-payments-roadmap/KPNG8v8NQLZM4mwD) was launched in April 2026, above a [national switch](https://itweb.africa/article/malawi-unveils-digital-payments-roadmap/KPNG8v8NQLZM4mwD) operating since 2015. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The strategy prioritises infrastructure expansion and affordability for low-income users, cybersecurity and resilience to power and connectivity failure, regulatory modernisation, financial technology innovation and regional integration, with the deputy governor acknowledging persistent rural gaps; no targets, budget or milestone dates are stated and the document itself is not held ([strategy](https://itweb.africa/article/malawi-unveils-digital-payments-roadmap/KPNG8v8NQLZM4mwD)).<br><br>The switch has linked banks, mobile money platforms and cash machines since 2015, with an instant bank transfer service live on it from June 2025, real-time to any Malawian bank up to MWK 500m by short code ([transfer](https://malawi24.com/2025/06/17/paychangu-centenary-bank-launch-instant-bank-transfer-to-transform-business-payments-in-malawi/), [infrastructure](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/solutions/malawian-fintech-paychangu-targets-sme-growth-with-unified-payment-infrastructure)).</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | A [National Fintech Strategy 2026-2030](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/mauritius-unveils-national-fintech-strategy-to-modernize-payments) was presented in June 2026, a [governance committee](https://maurice-info.mu/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/The-Economic-and-Financial-Measures-Miscellaneous-Provisions-Bill.pdf) put on a statutory footing, and a [digital onboarding system](https://lexpress.mu/node/554033) is under construction. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The strategy runs six pillars carrying a governance committee, open banking, an artificial-intelligence push in finance and a business facilitation bill, implemented with the central bank ([strategy](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/mauritius-unveils-national-fintech-strategy-to-modernize-payments)).<br><br>The committee is provided for in a new part of the financial services statute from September 2026, minister-chaired, seating the central bank governor, the financial regulator's chief executive, two ministries, the investment board and four private-sector members, empowered to approve sector-wide standards and expressly not a body corporate ([committee](https://maurice-info.mu/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/The-Economic-and-Financial-Measures-Miscellaneous-Provisions-Bill.pdf), [account](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/mauritius-unveils-national-fintech-strategy-to-modernize-payments)).<br><br>A know-your-customer system for digital onboarding is under construction alongside alignment to an international cross-border roadmap and exploratory work on fraud detection, with no delivery date published ([onboarding](https://lexpress.mu/node/554033)).</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | The central bank issued its [first standalone annual financial-inclusion report](https://www.bkam.ma/content/download/844040/9139542/Rapport%20annuel%20sur%20l'Inclusion%20financière%202024.pdf) in April 2026, separate from the strategy implementation series it used to sit inside. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The report is explicitly separate from the national inclusion strategy's implementation series, which makes measurement a standing annual duty of the bank rather than a by-product of a programme ([report](https://www.bkam.ma/content/download/844040/9139542/Rapport%20annuel%20sur%20l'Inclusion%20financière%202024.pdf)).<br><br>It names usage rather than access as what now limits inclusion, which is a judgement about the market and not only a count of it. No supervisory decision, licensing action or payment-system rule of the bank is separately held in this base.</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | A [National Payments System Law](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-cybercrime-and-cybersecurity-laws-national-payments-system-law-signed-into-force/) replaced the 2008 regime in July 2026, and a [notice](https://www.bancomoc.mz/media/wztpx2vc/aviso-2-gbm-2026-de-2-de-junho-regras-de-liga%C3%A7%C3%A3o-%C3%A0-rede-%C3%BAnica-de-pagamentos.pdf) makes switch connection mandatory with sixty-minute outage reporting. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The law was enacted in July 2026, extending central bank inspection and supervision over electronic-money institutions and new payment service providers; the official text is not held and its provisions rest on secondary accounts ([law](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-cybercrime-and-cybersecurity-laws-national-payments-system-law-signed-into-force/), [passage](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-national-payments-system-law-unanimously-approved-by-parliament/)).<br><br>The notice makes connection to the single payments network mandatory to offer electronic payment products, with three months to migrate existing terminals, any unavailability lasting more than ten minutes reported within sixty minutes, annual recovery testing due 31 March and an annual incident-framework review ([notice](https://www.bancomoc.mz/media/wztpx2vc/aviso-2-gbm-2026-de-2-de-junho-regras-de-liga%C3%A7%C3%A3o-%C3%A0-rede-%C3%BAnica-de-pagamentos.pdf), [reporting](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-financial-institutions-have-60-minutes-to-report-failures/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | The [Payment System Management Act 14 of 2023](https://www.bon.com.na/CMSTemplates/Bon/Files/bon.com.na/94/94193758-e15b-4af9-8321-4801142c82f7.pdf) is in force, [licensing is moving to the central bank](https://thebrief.com.na/2026/07/namibia-becomes-fourth-african-country-to-launch-instant-payments-system/), and a [vision to 2030](https://www.namibiansun.com/mw-main/namibia-moves-closer-to-instant-payments-NMH014446-3015-17893) succeeds a strategy that [closed at 87 per cent implementation](https://www.namibiansun.com/mw-main/namibia-moves-closer-to-instant-payments-NMH014446-3015-17893). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Act commenced in August 2023 and the payments association moved from a management council to a management board under it during 2025 ([Act](https://www.bon.com.na/CMSTemplates/Bon/Files/bon.com.na/94/94193758-e15b-4af9-8321-4801142c82f7.pdf), [board](https://thebrief.com.na/2026/07/namibia-becomes-fourth-african-country-to-launch-instant-payments-system/)).<br><br>Licensing and supervision of payment providers are to move to the central bank, with the association given independent governance and its own funding model; no instrument, commencement date or transition plan is published for the transfer ([transfer](https://thebrief.com.na/2026/07/namibia-becomes-fourth-african-country-to-launch-instant-payments-system/)). Licensing itself already sits with the bank: a determination on the licensing and authorisation of payment service providers took effect on 15 February 2024, setting governance, capital, risk and consumer-protection requirements for bank and non-bank providers alike ([determination](https://www.lac.org.na/laws/2024/8308.pdf)). The bank went to tender in November 2025 for a reconciliation and attestation tool for the instant payment operator, with no award on record ([tender](https://www.bon.com.na/CMSTemplates/Bon/Files/bon.com.na/e4/e4467afa-167e-4773-aa09-b1c286d25223.pdf)).<br><br>The 2021-2025 strategy closed at an 87 per cent average implementation rate over five years ([strategy](https://thebrief.com.na/2026/07/namibia-becomes-fourth-african-country-to-launch-instant-payments-system/)), succeeded by a vision to 2030 whose next phase is in drafting on interoperable instant payments, the quick-response standard, open banking and cyber resilience ([vision](https://thebrief.com.na/2026/07/namibia-becomes-fourth-african-country-to-launch-instant-payments-system/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | A [Payments System Vision 2028](https://www.cbn.gov.ng/Out/2026/CCD/Nigeria%20Payments%20System%20Vision%202028.pdf) sets 95 per cent inclusion and a 70 per cent fraud cut from a ₦1.2 quadrillion baseline, alongside [market-concentration caps](https://techcabal.com/2026/07/20/nigerias-central-bank-is-rewriting-the-rules-for-fintech-growth/), a [foreign-exchange tracker](https://itweb.africa/article/nigeria-central-bank-digitises-forex-oversight/KPNG878N9pYq4mwD) and a [second sandbox cohort](https://www.itweb.africa/article/nigeria-opens-second-sandbox-cohort/KWEBb7yLbGmvmRjO). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The vision runs a three-year horizon from 2025 baselines of ₦1.2 quadrillion in electronic transactions, 5.9m active terminals and 69.5m unique identifiers, targeting 95 per cent financial inclusion, full interoperability, adult digital-payment usage from about 52 to 80 per cent, 5m code-based merchant outlets and a 70 per cent reduction in fraud losses ([vision](https://www.cbn.gov.ng/Out/2026/CCD/Nigeria%20Payments%20System%20Vision%202028.pdf), [launch](https://www.cbn.gov.ng/PaymentsSystem/PSV2028.html)).<br><br>Any institution above 25 per cent of consumer issuing may not exceed 15 per cent of merchant acquiring and the reverse, on monthly market-share returns with compliance by end-2026 ([caps](https://techcabal.com/2026/07/20/nigerias-central-bank-is-rewriting-the-rules-for-fintech-growth/)).<br><br>Every licensed foreign-exchange bureau must report purchases in real time or same-day with banks as first line of enforcement ([tracker](https://itweb.africa/article/nigeria-central-bank-digitises-forex-oversight/KPNG878N9pYq4mwD)); a second sandbox cohort opened to virtual-asset and data-enabled firms ([sandbox](https://www.itweb.africa/article/nigeria-opens-second-sandbox-cohort/KWEBb7yLbGmvmRjO)); and the retail digital currency is being redesigned toward a wholesale model rather than abandoned ([currency](https://thenationonlineng.net/payment-vision-2028-is-nigeria-ready-for-digital-economy/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | All domestic interoperable retail payments [migrated to a national instant payment system](https://itweb.africa/article/rwanda-migrates-retail-payments-to-ekash/rxP3jqBEnOoMA2ye) by directive in July 2026, and the [interoperable transfer fee](https://rba.rw/a-fully-connected-rwanda-what-ekash-interoperability-means-for-the-future-of-financial-inclusion/) is a ceiling of RWF 20 rather than a flat charge. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Consumer launch was December 2025 and interoperability completed in July 2026, when a directive migrated all domestic interoperable retail payments onto the system ([migration](https://itweb.africa/article/rwanda-migrates-retail-payments-to-ekash/rxP3jqBEnOoMA2ye), [interoperability](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/rwanda-launches-ekash-to-enable-nationwide-instant-interoperable-payments), [design](https://mojaloop.io/how-mojaloop-enables-rndps-2-0-ekash/)).<br><br>The fee is a maximum, not a flat charge, and it prices the interoperability layer only: the bankers' association's public notice puts it as a maximum with institutions free to offer lower or zero-fee options, applying to every transfer between banks and electronic money issuers ([fee](https://rba.rw/a-fully-connected-rwanda-what-ekash-interoperability-means-for-the-future-of-financial-inclusion/), [performance](https://www.bnr.rw/documents/Press_Release-_Update_on_the_Performance_of_eKash_Aug_2026.pdf)).<br><br>The governing directive is not held: the central bank's document library was enumerated and carries neither it nor the public notice, and two sources give different issue dates for it, so its date is unestablished. What governs on-net tariffs is likewise unestablished ([scrutiny](https://taarifa.rw/2026/08/02/mtn-mobile-money-faces-scrutiny-over-fees-as-rwandas-digital-economy-expands/), [launch](https://african.business/2026/07/innov-africa-deals/rwanda-unifies-digital-payments-with-national-launch-of-ekash)).</details> | Advanced |
| Sao Tome and Principe | The central bank [launched a first financial technology sandbox](https://rstp.st/2025/06/03/banco-central-lanca-iniciativa-sandbox-para-promover-inclusao-de-servicos-financeiros-digitais-em-stp/) under the national financial inclusion strategy, and no later update is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The sandbox was launched in June 2025 ([sandbox](https://rstp.st/2025/06/03/banco-central-lanca-iniciativa-sandbox-para-promover-inclusao-de-servicos-financeiros-digitais-em-stp/)).<br><br>No cohort, admission, criteria or graduation is recorded since. It is the only payments instrument in this ledger apart from a donor grant active since 2017, so the country's entire digital payments record is a sandbox with no published cohort and a grant with an unexplained disbursement figure.</details> | No change |
| Seychelles | The central bank [set a four-stage cheque phase-out](http://www.seychellesnewsagency.com/articles/21244/seychelles-to-phase-out-cheques-for-financial-transactions-from-2025) running to May 2026, under [electronic money regulations](https://www.gazette.sc/sites/default/files/2023-01/SI%20128%202022%20-%20National%20Payment%20System%20%28Electronic%20Money%29%20Regulations%2C%202022.pdf) in force since 2022, and Cabinet [approved widening the authorised-dealer perimeter](https://statehouse.gov.sc/cabinet-decisions/7151/cabinet-business-thursday-23rd-july-2026). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The phase-out runs in four dated stages: stop issuing to individuals from January 2025, stop accepting from individuals from May 2025, stop issuing to non-residents from January 2026, stop accepting from non-residents after May 2026. The cheque-volume and card figures behind it carry a proxy date caveat ([phase-out](http://www.seychellesnewsagency.com/articles/21244/seychelles-to-phase-out-cheques-for-financial-transactions-from-2025), [schedule](https://nation.sc/articles/31435/src-introduces-new-cheque-payment-limits-plans-full-phase-out-by-2028)).<br><br>The electronic money regulations were made under the payment system Act in 2022, giving incumbents six months to comply and a year to reach the capital floor; they were acquired from the Official Gazette only in August 2026, so they are a foundational text newly added to this base rather than a new development ([regulations](https://www.gazette.sc/sites/default/files/2023-01/SI%20128%202022%20-%20National%20Payment%20System%20%28Electronic%20Money%29%20Regulations%2C%202022.pdf)).<br><br>Cabinet approved amendments widening the authorised-dealer definition to include credit unions and licensed payment service providers ([perimeter](https://statehouse.gov.sc/cabinet-decisions/7151/cabinet-business-thursday-23rd-july-2026)). A dated, staged withdrawal of an instrument is the most orderly payments transition in this frame.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | The central bank set out the [bank integration sequence for the instant payment system](https://sonna.so/en/article/Central-Bank-sets-out-plan-to-bring-banks-and-mobile-money-onto-one-payments-network) in July 2026, on a switch it deliberately chose to run as a joint venture rather than as central-bank infrastructure. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The sequence was set out on 26 July 2026 ([sequence](https://sonna.so/en/article/Central-Bank-sets-out-plan-to-bring-banks-and-mobile-money-onto-one-payments-network), [expansion](https://www.hiiraan.com/news4/2026/Jul/205808/somalia_to_expand_instant_payment_system_linking_banks_and_mobile_money_platform.aspx), [launch](https://www.fintechfutures.com/instant-real-time-payments/somalia-taps-bpc-to-launch-first-nationwide-instant-payment-system)).<br><br>The switch is a joint venture of the central bank and thirteen commercial banks, which the governor described as a deliberate choice over central-bank-run infrastructure. A national code-based payment standard is linked to it as the national standard ([standard](https://sonna.so/en/article/Central-Bank-sets-out-plan-to-bring-banks-and-mobile-money-onto-one-payments-network)).<br><br>No transaction volume, participant count or go-live date for the full sequence is published.</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | The central bank [ruled out non-bank shareholding in the payments utility it half-owns](https://www.businessday.co.za/business-times/2026-08-01-no-plans-to-add-non-banks-to-payinc-shareholding-kganyago/) and [restated that a retail digital currency has no compelling case](https://www.mondaq.com/southafrica/financial-services/1801688/payments-first-why-the-sarb-is-prioritising-digital-infrastructure-over-a-retail-cbdc). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-31 — the Governor told the Bank's ordinary general meeting that it [would have preferred to hold all of the payments utility and is not looking at bringing others in](https://www.businessday.co.za/business-times/2026-08-01-no-plans-to-add-non-banks-to-payinc-shareholding-kganyago/), having acquired 50% in October 2025. The installed base around it was put at 39 participating banks and more than 480 non-bank payment service providers.<br><br>2026-06 — on digital currency the position hardened rather than moved: the Bank's published view is that [a retail central bank digital currency has no compelling case yet and the everyday rails come first](https://www.mondaq.com/southafrica/financial-services/1801688/payments-first-why-the-sarb-is-prioritising-digital-infrastructure-over-a-retail-cbdc), with its wholesale distributed-ledger work confined to two completed projects.<br><br>Both are the same posture from two directions — the central bank taking ownership of the shared retail rail while declining to issue a retail instrument of its own.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | The central bank [affirmed mobile money as legal tender](https://www.onecitizendaily.com/index.php/2025/07/19/central-bank-affirms-mobile-money-as-legal-tender/), and the [national instant payment system](https://africanenda.org/bank-of-south-sudan-launches-the-countrys-first-national-instant-payment-system/) has been at roadmap stage since early 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The affirmation was made under a 2017 electronic money regulation; the target of 30 per cent adult usage by 2027 is the central bank's own, and economists called the declaration premature in an economy over 90 per cent cash-based ([affirmation](https://www.onecitizendaily.com/index.php/2025/07/19/central-bank-affirms-mobile-money-as-legal-tender/), [reactions](https://www.onecitizendaily.com/index.php/2025/07/22/central-banks-push-for-mobile-money-as-legal-tender-triggers-mixed-reactions/)).<br><br>Roadmap development for the instant payment system was announced in February 2025 after a 2023 proof of concept, and nothing has moved since ([roadmap](https://africanenda.org/bank-of-south-sudan-launches-the-countrys-first-national-instant-payment-system/)).<br><br>A declaration of legal tender ahead of the rail that would clear it is the sequencing this unit repeats across identity, payments and law.</details> | Mixed, mobile money affirmed as legal tender while the instant payment rail that would carry it has not moved since 2025 |
| Sudan | The [national payment switch](https://www.suna.sd/posts/albnk-almrkzy-ydshn-khdmat-almhol-alkomy) launched with one bank connected, and a [rival payment-switch licence was revoked](https://sudantribune.net/article/315827) days after that platform's launch with no breach disclosed. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The central bank launched an electronic capital-flows system on 26 August 2026, replacing manual processing ([launch](https://thesudantimes.com/sudan/central-bank-of-sudan-launches-electronic-capital-flows-system/)).<br><br>The switch went live connecting one bank as its first and only participant, with the first cash-machine withdrawal completed over it; the central bank's own framing is restoration rather than a greenfield rail ([switch](https://www.suna.sd/posts/albnk-almrkzy-ydshn-khdmat-almhol-alkomy), [tender](https://cbos.gov.sd/en/content/tender-announcement-national-instant-payment-system-project-nips)).<br><br>A separate payment-switch licence was revoked days after the platform launched, with no specific breach disclosed; a Gulf linkage was reported by domestic outlets and not confirmed by the central bank ([revocation](https://sudantribune.net/article/315827)).<br><br>A regulator that licenses and then revokes without stating a reason, and simultaneously operates the competing rail, is a market-structure fact the record states without explaining.</details> | Advanced |
| Tanzania | The central bank's [sandbox reviewed 35 applications and approved five](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026072316300213.pdf) for live testing, launched a [sovereign yield curve system](https://www.mwananchi.co.tz/mw/habari/biashara/bot-yaja-na-mfumo-kuongeza-wawekeza-dhamana-za-serikali-5549792), and a [digital currency remains under study](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026072316300213.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The sandbox throughput comes from an annual report; the sandbox existed earlier but no earlier throughput figures are held ([sandbox](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026072316300213.pdf)).<br><br>The yield curve system was launched in August 2026, with the investor counts the governor's own given at the launch ([yield curve](https://www.mwananchi.co.tz/mw/habari/biashara/bot-yaja-na-mfumo-kuongeza-wawekeza-dhamana-za-serikali-5549792)).<br><br>A central bank digital currency remains under study with no design, pilot or timetable in any held source ([currency](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026072316300213.pdf)), and a national code payment standard had 27 participating providers at end-2025 ([standard](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026060211494565.pdf)).</details> | Advanced |
| Tunisia | The central bank [launched a national payment label](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/tunisia-launches-tunpay-label-to-accelerate-mobile-payment-adoption) with the national payments operator, [licensed an operator fintech wallet](https://menastartupdigest.com/ooredoo-fintech-secures-license-to-launch-digital-wallet-in-tunisia/), and the state operator [launched a wallet](https://www.assabahnews.tn/ar/%D8%A7%D8%AE%D8%A8%D8%A7%D8%B1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%A4%D8%B3%D8%B3%D8%A7%D8%AA/142463-%D8%A7%D8%AA%D8%B5%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D8%AA%D9%88%D9%86%D8%B3-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D9%84%D9%82-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D8%A8%D9%8A%D9%82%D9%87%D8%A7-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%AF%D9%81%D8%B9-%D8%B9%D8%A8%D8%B1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%87%D8%A7%D8%AA%D9%81-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84-%D9%83%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%8A) of its own. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The payment label was launched in May 2026 with the national payments operator ([label](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/tunisia-launches-tunpay-label-to-accelerate-mobile-payment-adoption)).<br><br>An operator fintech wallet received its central bank licence in March 2026 ([licence](https://menastartupdigest.com/ooredoo-fintech-secures-license-to-launch-digital-wallet-in-tunisia/)), and the state operator launched a wallet in the same month; the base's capture of the only source no longer carries the article, so the launch detail is not currently citable beyond the headline ([wallet](https://www.assabahnews.tn/ar/%D8%A7%D8%AE%D8%A8%D8%A7%D8%B1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%A4%D8%B3%D8%B3%D8%A7%D8%AA/142463-%D8%A7%D8%AA%D8%B5%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%A7%D8%AA-%D8%AA%D9%88%D9%86%D8%B3-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D9%84%D9%82-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D8%A8%D9%8A%D9%82%D9%87%D8%A7-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF-%D9%84%D9%84%D8%AF%D9%81%D8%B9-%D8%B9%D8%A8%D8%B1-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%87%D8%A7%D8%AA%D9%81-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AC%D9%88%D8%A7%D9%84-%D9%83%D8%A7%D8%B4%D9%8A)).<br><br>Two operator wallets licensed or launched in one month, under a new national label, is a coordinated opening of the payments market rather than three separate events.</details> | Advanced |
| Uganda | [Cash-withdrawal caps](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/bou-sets-cash-withdrawal-limits-for-individua-NV_234854_062026) take effect on 1 January 2027 for a cash-lite economy, while the [national payment switch](https://ubc.go.ug/2025/10/06/bous-new-payment-system-cuts-delays-and-boosts-e-money-by-28-6) and the [settlement-system replacement](https://ubc.go.ug/2025/10/06/bous-new-payment-system-cuts-delays-and-boosts-e-money-by-28-6) are unmoved. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The caps were issued by circular in May 2026 for a January 2027 start, the stated purpose being a cash-lite economy ([caps](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/bou-sets-cash-withdrawal-limits-for-individua-NV_234854_062026)).<br><br>The switch was reported in progress in the central bank's 2024/25 account and has not moved ([switch](https://ubc.go.ug/2025/10/06/bous-new-payment-system-cuts-delays-and-boosts-e-money-by-28-6)). The settlement replacement and standards migration had a stated go-live of November 2025 which passed unverified; absence of confirmation is not evidence of non-delivery ([settlement](https://ubc.go.ug/2025/10/06/bous-new-payment-system-cuts-delays-and-boosts-e-money-by-28-6)).<br><br>A digital shilling pilot was announced inside a US$5.5bn settlement-network plan in October 2025 with nothing since ([pilot](https://techcabal.com/2025/10/08/uganda-digital-infrastructure-karamoja/)). Capping cash before the rail that would replace it is confirmed working is the sequence.</details> | Mixed, cash-withdrawal caps set for 2027 while the payment switch and the settlement-system replacement both stand unmoved |
| Zambia | The [national financial switch](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/from-rails-to-routine/dgp45vaB8nQ7X9l8) has its cost-reduction objective stated on record as not yet delivered, and [cheque acceptance is to stop](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/from-rails-to-routine/dgp45vaB8nQ7X9l8) on a date sourced from a single sentence about another country. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The electronic clearing house is stated as not yet live on the regional instant-payment scheme, and central bank interest in the continental system is characterised as conversation rather than commitment ([switch](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/from-rails-to-routine/dgp45vaB8nQ7X9l8)).<br><br>The cheque stop date comes from a single sentence in an article about another country, and no central bank circular is held ([cheques](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/from-rails-to-routine/dgp45vaB8nQ7X9l8), [comparator](https://itweb.africa/article/the-rise-of-cheques-as-kenyas-preferred-payment-method/wbrpOqg2aBXMDLZn)).<br><br>Ending an instrument on a date evidenced only in foreign coverage is a limit on what this report can state about the transition.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | The central bank [established a digital transformation and financial technology department](https://technomag.co.zw/rbz-unveils-dedicated-department-for-digital-transformation-and-fintech-innovation/) with a sandbox and an innovation hub, and publishes a [quarterly national payment systems report](https://www.rbz.co.zw/documents/nps/quarterly/2026/NPSD_SECOND_QUARTER_REPORT_ACTIVITY_JUNE_2026.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The department was announced by a deputy governor at a technology forum; no separate central bank document is held ([department](https://technomag.co.zw/rbz-unveils-dedicated-department-for-digital-transformation-and-fintech-innovation/), [forum](https://technomag.co.zw/rbz-unveils-dedicated-department-for-digital-transformation-and-fintech-innovation/)).<br><br>The payment systems report is a first-party quarterly series; the first-quarter 2026 issue is not held, and the quarter-on-quarter comparison carried here is the second-quarter report's own ([report](https://www.rbz.co.zw/documents/nps/quarterly/2026/NPSD_SECOND_QUARTER_REPORT_ACTIVITY_JUNE_2026.pdf), [prior](https://www.rbz.co.zw/documents/nps/quarterly/2025/NPSD_FOURTH_QUARTER_REPORT_ACTIVITY_DECEMBER_2025.pdf)).<br><br>Mobile money and interbank transfer limits were raised in the 2026 monetary policy statement, the bank stating the rise is intended to widen local-currency usage ([limits](https://www.newsday.co.zw/local-news/article/200052094/rbz-cuts-limits-bank-charges-expands-mobile-and-zipit-limits)).</details> | Advanced |

## G2P functionality

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The pension fund [replaced the paper life certificate with facial recognition](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/societe/mp2vxd49-le-certificat-de-vie-remplace-par-la-reconnaissance-faciale) and credited arrears to [more than 3.5 million accounts](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/societe/mooh4njn-versement-ce-samedi-des-augmentations-pour-plus-de-3-5-millions-de-retraites-et-ayants-droit). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-05-12 - [the pension fund replaced the paper life certificate with a facial-recognition verification system for pensioners](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/societe/mp2vxd49-le-certificat-de-vie-remplace-par-la-reconnaissance-faciale) - a biometric check substituting for an in-person one, with no accuracy, exception or appeal route published.<br><br>2026-05-02 - [pension-increase arrears were credited to the accounts of more than 3.5 million retirees and beneficiaries](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/societe/mooh4njn-versement-ce-samedi-des-augmentations-pour-plus-de-3-5-millions-de-retraites-et-ayants-droit), on a [partnership the fund signed with a state bank in January 2026](https://www.aps.dz/fr/algerie/societe/mjv9qrlt-signature-d-une-convention-de-partenariat-entre-la-cnr-et-la-bdl).<br><br>The other transfer on the record is [a solidarity allowance and its beneficiary framework, established by decree in October 2024](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2024/F2024068.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Angola | The cash-transfer programme [reached more than 1.3 million households and about 6.5 million citizens](https://angop.ao/noticias/sociedade/lancamento-do-cadastro-social-unico-marca-a-semana-social/), against 247,000 households at the prior position. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-25 — the programme [is reported at more than 1.3 million households and about 6.5 million citizens reached, with a single social register now the centralised targeting database](https://angop.ao/noticias/sociedade/lancamento-do-cadastro-social-unico-marca-a-semana-social/), against a starting position of [a US$320m loan building the social registry and government-to-person rails, with more than 247,000 households reached against a 1.6 million family target](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/609711563588128020/pdf/Angola-Strengthening-the-National-Social-Protection-System-Project.pdf).<br><br>That is the largest measured government-to-person payment operation on this ledger by an order of magnitude. What is not published is the payment instrument: no source states how the transfers reach households, what share is paid into a wallet or an account rather than in cash, or what it costs to deliver.<br><br>No data-protection basis or governing instrument was stated for the register at launch, which is reported under registries.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | A productive safety net [began cash transfers in September 2025 to 20,621 beneficiaries, 85% of them women](https://www.gouv.bj/article/3265/mise-oeuvre-programme-gbessoke-transferts-monetaires-demarre-plus-mille-menages-deja-touches-/), onto free secured SIM cards. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-09-18 — [cash transfers began to 20,621 beneficiaries, 85% of them women, paid a first monthly instalment of 208,272,100 FCFA across twelve pilot communes onto free secured SIM cards, ahead of an intended extension to all 77 communes](https://www.gouv.bj/article/3265/mise-oeuvre-programme-gbessoke-transferts-monetaires-demarre-plus-mille-menages-deja-touches-/). Eligibility was certified house by house by the statistics institute and the social affairs ministry.<br><br>Nothing existed before it and no extension beyond the twelve pilot communes has been reported in the eleven months since. The house-by-house certification is the interesting detail: the targeting was done on foot, and the single social register created four months later, reported under registries, is the instrument intended to replace that method.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | Biometric grant cards [stand at more than 75,000 beneficiaries and 1,200 shops](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202505/biometric-payment-card-system-pushing-financial-inclusion-in-botswana), the same figure as a year ago, and [the announced citizen wallet has had no follow-up](https://techafricanews.com/2025/09/17/botswana-partners-with-pemandu-to-drive-nationwide-digital-transformation/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The biometric grant payment system reached more than 75,000 beneficiaries and 1,200 shops on a platform accredited by three central banks](https://techafricanews.com/2025/05/28/smartswitch-botswana-pioneers-dignified-digital-aid-for-75000-beneficiaries/), [and the same figure is all the base holds](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202505/biometric-payment-card-system-pushing-financial-inclusion-in-botswana). No update has been published in the twelve months, and the figure is restated elsewhere under a 2026 heading where it reads as current.<br><br>2025-09-17 — [a unified Citizen Wallet for subsidy delivery was presented to a rural development council alongside a cryptographic card](https://techafricanews.com/2025/09/17/botswana-partners-with-pemandu-to-drive-nationwide-digital-transformation/). Nothing further has appeared in ten months on either.<br><br>So government-to-person payment in Botswana is one closed-loop card scheme whose numbers have not moved, and a wallet that exists in a presentation.</details> | No change |
| Burundi | Social transfers [reach recipients over their own mobile phones](https://www.sosmediasburundi.org/2025/09/22/buhumuza-clap-de-fin-pour-merankabandi-deux-annees-de-transferts-monetaires-qui-ont-transforme-la-vie-des-refugies/), and the beneficiary system is now [connected to several payment providers](https://blogs.worldbank.org/en/nasikiliza/burundi-cash-for-jobs-project-is-embracing-smart-it-for-social-impact). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-09 — [a cash-transfer programme closed in one province after two years, having paid 73,600 BIF every two months over recipients' own mobile phones, 72,000 BIF net of the withdrawal charge](https://www.sosmediasburundi.org/2025/09/22/buhumuza-clap-de-fin-pour-merankabandi-deux-annees-de-transferts-monetaires-qui-ont-transforme-la-vie-des-refugies/). It is the clearest record the base holds of a government-to-person digital channel reaching a specific population, and the withdrawal charge is the part worth keeping: the recipient pays to take the money out.<br><br>2026-01 — [the successor system migrated more than a million individual beneficiary records and was connected to several payment providers](https://blogs.worldbank.org/en/nasikiliza/burundi-cash-for-jobs-project-is-embracing-smart-it-for-social-impact), which is the structural change: payment routed through a system rather than through one programme's arrangement.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | Officials outside the capital [are paid by mobile money and went unpaid for a month when one operator's network failed](https://corbeaunews-centrafrique.org/centrafrique-a-batangafo-un-mois-sans-reseau-orange-bloque-les-communications-et-les-services-financiers/), against [1,702 civil servants enrolled in May 2025](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099052925135520222/pdf/BOSIB-50d33b34-4918-409e-92f7-070a1773ac56.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[1,702 civil servants in regions without banking services were being paid by mobile money, recorded as a project achievement in May 2025](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099052925135520222/pdf/BOSIB-50d33b34-4918-409e-92f7-070a1773ac56.pdf). No later enrolment figure is held.<br><br>2026-07-28 — [at one town subscribers reported no calls, no internet and no mobile money for close to a month, with prepaid bundles expiring unused and some officials unpaid for the previous month](https://corbeaunews-centrafrique.org/centrafrique-a-batangafo-un-mois-sans-reseau-orange-bloque-les-communications-et-les-services-financiers/). Single-operator dependency with no redundancy is the mechanism.<br><br>A payroll rail that fails for a month is not a payroll rail. The state has moved salaries onto a private network in places where it has no alternative, and nothing on this ledger records a fallback, a service-level obligation or a redundancy requirement on the operator.</details> | Stalled |
| Comoros | [40,010 households across about 196 villages are paid up to 75,000 francs through mobile banking](https://africa24tv.com/comores-financement-des-filets-sociaux-plus-de-40-000-menages-vulnerables-beneficiaires/) under the safety-nets programme. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The rails were commissioned first: the central bank's financial inclusion project, [closed on 30 June 2025, commissioned the automated transfer system and the interoperable payment switch](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099032326091520607/pdf/BOSIB-cd362a8b-2323-4f0d-8e18-4073ca0c5ea8.pdf).<br><br>2025-12 - the supervision mission [credited the digitisation of operations, the use of electronic payments and a digitised monitoring system with the project's delivery, disbursement having reached 82.8 per cent, and recorded remediation of delayed mobile-money payments to beneficiaries on Anjouan](https://pfss-comores.org/ressources/download/9).<br><br>2026-03 - the programme was [publicly put at 40,010 vulnerable households in about 196 villages across the three islands, each eligible for up to 75,000 Comorian francs paid by mobile banking, under a 30 million US dollar financing](https://africa24tv.com/comores-financement-des-filets-sociaux-plus-de-40-000-menages-vulnerables-beneficiaires/).<br><br>This is the only government-to-person channel in the base with a beneficiary count behind it.</details> | Advanced |
| Congo | The public service minister disclosed [fraudulent duplicate payroll numbers and deceased or retired staff still drawing salaries](https://brazzavilleinsider.com/congo-bets-on-digital-records-to-curb-civil-fraud/) to the Senate. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-07 - the minister [disclosed to the Senate that fraudulent duplicate payroll numbers exist and that deceased or retired staff are still drawing salaries](https://brazzavilleinsider.com/congo-bets-on-digital-records-to-curb-civil-fraud/). No count, value, remediation date or incident detail is stated, and the disclosure is the minister's own.<br><br>The platform itself is running, with [three standing user complaints recorded by the ministry that operates it](https://www.finances.gouv.cg/fr/articles/s%C3%A9ance-de-travail-sur-e-solde_200326). A payroll system in production whose operator minutes three failure modes and whose supervising minister reports fraud on it to the legislature is the position as the base holds it, with no audit, no count of affected agents and no remedy on file.</details> | Regressed |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [treasury payments platform was extended toward local authorities, with 75 collectivities and 4,500 distributors claimed](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2640916/societe/collectivites-territoriales-clotchor-secongo-developpe-un-systeme-pour-digitaliser-les-services). <details><summary>Full record</summary>At the window's opening the platform was [in operation for public-expenditure and government-to-person flows, with the expenditure module under optimisation](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2636137/economie/cote-divoire-le-tresor-public-accelere-la-digitalisation-des-depenses-publiques-avec-tresorpaytresormoney).<br><br>2026-03-27 - it was [reported extended toward local authorities, with 75 collectivities and 4,500 distributors claimed](https://www.fratmat.info/article/2640916/societe/collectivites-territoriales-clotchor-secongo-developpe-un-systeme-pour-digitaliser-les-services). Those figures come from the developer's own press note, not from the treasury.<br><br>No payment volume, value, beneficiary count or failure rate is published for any government-to-person flow, so the platform's reach is described only by the number of institutions attached to it.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | The [state personnel file was ordered updated by article 32 of the 2026 budget law](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-de-finances-n203-an-25-9eme-l-portant-budget-initial-de-letat-pour-lexercice-2026/) to integrate contract staff and unregularised headcounts. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The file existed and its position at the window's opening is not held. The [2026 budget law orders the budget ministry, jointly with the labour ministry charged with administrative reform, to update the database to integrate contract staff and headcounts awaiting regularisation, with no completion date, budget line or headcount stated](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-de-finances-n203-an-25-9eme-l-portant-budget-initial-de-letat-pour-lexercice-2026/).<br><br>A payroll database that a finance law has to order updated to include the staff already being paid from it is the position as the base holds it. No count of records, no audit and no remediation report follows it on file.</details> | Advanced |
| DR Congo | A [rural payroll arrangement was scoped to three territories](https://acp.cd/economie/rdc-harmonisation-technique-pour-lancer-la-paie-des-fonctionnaires-en-milieux-ruraux/), while [Kinshasa agents demonstrated over two months of arrears](https://lepotentiel.cd/2026/07/23/kinshasa-les-agents-de-ladministration-publique-urbaine-reclament-le-paiement-de-deux-mois-darrieres-de-salaires/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07 - a state bank and the payroll monitoring committee [settled technical and organisational terms for rural payroll, scoped to three named territories and others where no other bank operates, with August 2026 named as a target, the bank's director-general saying a fixed date is premature, the state contract unsigned and its core banking replacement still in test](https://actualite.cd/2026/07/24/paie-des-agents-publics-la-cadeco-et-le-csp-prets-lancer-la-paie-de-proximite-en-milieu). The [bank's reach is reported at both around 40 sites and about thirty territories](https://acp.cd/economie/rdc-harmonisation-technique-pour-lancer-la-paie-des-fonctionnaires-en-milieux-ruraux/), and that reach is the whole rationale for the arrangement.<br><br>2026-07-23 - Kinshasa urban administration agents [demonstrated over two months of unpaid salary, beneficiary listings already transmitted to the paying bank but neither bank staff nor payroll agents attending the appointed payment](https://lepotentiel.cd/2026/07/23/kinshasa-les-agents-de-ladministration-publique-urbaine-reclament-le-paiement-de-deux-mois-darrieres-de-salaires/) - a failure at the last mile of an otherwise complete digital chain.<br><br>The [biometric civil-servant registry stands at more than 118,000 cards across 26 provinces with no updated figure in twelve months](https://blogs.worldbank.org/en/nasikiliza/in-drc-public-administration-enters-a-new-era-with-a-digital-civil-servant-registry).</details> | Mixed, a rural payroll route was scoped while Kinshasa's urban administration went two months unpaid and the biometric civil-servant register published no new figure |
