| Egypt | A [completion report closes a US$900m safety-net loan](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099060624125531275/txt/BOSIB169cd52710ff1abfc17842717d1e22.txt); no current payment channel figure is held. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The arrangements are described in [a country note on government-to-person payments, which sets out the flagship cash transfer programme launched in 2015](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/533491624466169827/pdf/G2P-Payment-Arrangements-in-Egypt-and-COVID-19.pdf).<br><br>2024-06-05 - a completion report [closes a US$900m-equivalent loan strengthening the safety net](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099060624125531275/txt/BOSIB169cd52710ff1abfc17842717d1e22.txt).<br><br>Neither states a current beneficiary count, a payment channel split or a digital-payment share, so what proportion of transfers now reach people electronically is not on the base - against a [financial inclusion rate of 77.6% at the end of 2025](https://www.cbe.org.eg/en/news-publications/news/2026/02/17/14/44/financial-inclusion-2026).</details> | No change, a first stated position with no earlier account behind it |
| Equatorial Guinea | [Benefits are paid bank-only](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_delegado_nacional_del_inseso_comparece_ante_la_camara_de_los_diputados) and a 2025 census purged 2,282 untraceable pensioners. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [insurance institute's delegate told the Chamber of Deputies in September 2025 that social benefits are now paid only into bank accounts, and that a 2025 census purged 2,282 untraceable pensioners](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_delegado_nacional_del_inseso_comparece_ante_la_camara_de_los_diputados).<br><br>That is government-to-person payment through the banking system rather than a payment rail of the state's own. The [order banning cash collection](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/orden_de_la_presidencia_del_gobierno_relativa_a_la_recaudacion_o_cobro_en_efectivo_de_impuestos_tasas_y_demas_ingresos_de_la_administracion) covers the flow in the other direction. Payroll credibility is uneven: a bank was [ordered to repay 182m CFA to 12,000 civil servants over an unauthorised fee scheme](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/bange_devolvera_este_2_de_abril_182_millones_a_los_funcionarios_por_recortes).</details> | Advanced |
| Eritrea | Eritrea is absent from the continental inventory of instant payment systems, so no rail exists for government-to-person payment ([inventory](https://www.africanenda.org/uploads/files/siips_2024_mainreport_EN.1.pdf)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The annual continental inventory of live and planned instant payment systems, covering systems with live transactions as of June 2024, does not list Eritrea among the 31 operating systems across 26 countries, and does not identify it among those supporting government-to-person functionality ([inventory](https://www.africanenda.org/uploads/files/siips_2024_mainreport_EN.1.pdf)).<br><br>The financial system it would run on has no cash machines, cards or internet banking ([evaluation](https://www.esaamlg.org/reports/MER%20of%20Eritrea-July%202025.pdf)).<br><br>Government payments to people are therefore made in cash, and nothing on the base records a plan to change that.</details> | No change |
| Eswatini | A [social grant disbursement system](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/bank-paid-elderly-grants-shift-to-25th-monthly/) built with the science park moved bank-paid elderly grants to the 25th of the month from August 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The deputy prime minister's office introduced the system to make grant payment safer, faster and more reliable, shifting the bank-paid elderly grant date from the 20th to the 25th ([system](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/bank-paid-elderly-grants-shift-to-25th-monthly/)).<br><br>Its own annual report gives the operational split of elderly-grant payment between mobile money and bank accounts for April to December 2024 ([annual report](https://parliament.gov.sz/publications/parliament_reports/docs/ANNUAL%20REPORT%20DPMO%202025.pdf)).<br><br>35 post offices accept government-service payments, which is the counter-flow from citizen to state.<br><br>No disbursement value, recipient count or failure rate is published for the new system, and the social registry that would target it has covered one region of four.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | Finance, the central bank and the national switch convened government institutions to operationalise interoperable government payments ([report](https://www.findevgateway.org/news/ethiopia-advances-nationwide-rollout-of-interoperable-digital-government-payments)). <details><summary>Full record</summary>In July 2026 the finance ministry, the central bank and the national switch convened technical teams from government institutions to operationalise a unified implementation framework for interoperable person-to-government and government-to-person payments, aligned to the national digital payments strategy ([report](https://www.findevgateway.org/news/ethiopia-advances-nationwide-rollout-of-interoperable-digital-government-payments)).<br><br>The rails behind it were launched at the December 2025 payments conference, which recorded both the draft national digital payments strategy and the instant payment system ([conference](https://edpc.nbe.gov.et/)).<br><br>The national switch's own annual report for the year to June 2025 sets out its mandate over interoperability, the national payment scheme and the national payment gateway, and carries government payment work as a project under implementation rather than a live service ([annual report](https://ethswitch.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/10/ETS_Annual_Report_2024_2025.pdf)).<br><br>The base holds no volume or value of government payments actually made over these rails.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | A [blockchain payment-distribution pilot](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/undp-pushes-blockchain-from-dpi-pilots-to-deployment) is running with a United Nations agency, grouped with pilots in four other countries. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The agency reports distribution costs cut from about 10 per cent to as little as 2 per cent across the five pilots, an aggregate figure not disaggregated for this country ([pilots](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202608/undp-pushes-blockchain-from-dpi-pilots-to-deployment)).<br><br>No country-specific volume, beneficiary count or launch date is published, and the agency names governance, interoperability and trust as the remaining barriers before country-programme integration.</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | The cash-grant programme paid [GH¢485m in the first half of 2026](https://www.mofep.gov.gh/sites/default/files/budget-statements/2026-Mid-Year-Fiscal-Policy-Review-Speech.pdf) on mobile-money rails, against a full-year allocation of GH¢1.1bn. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The allocation is the highest since inception ([fiscal review](https://www.mofep.gov.gh/sites/default/files/budget-statements/2026-Mid-Year-Fiscal-Policy-Review-Speech.pdf)).<br><br>A recent cycle released about GH¢139m to roughly 350,580 households ([payment cycle](https://www.mogcsp.gov.gh/government-begins-99th-cycle-of-leap-cash-grant-payments)), and the stated expansion to 400,000 households in 2026 has no delivery figure on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | The blockchain wage bill and pensions platform remains the only government-to-person rail described. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Nothing in this batch adds to the government-to-person picture beyond the wage bill and pensions platform the ledger already carries.<br><br>What the window does establish is that the rails around it are thin: [one licensed payment institution and one electronic money issuer in the whole country](https://www.bceao.int/sites/default/files/inline-files/4%20-%20Liste%20Guin%C3%A9e-Bissau_2026.pdf) as at June 2026, and [only 22 per cent of women holding a bank or mobile money account](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099037006302214402/pdf/IDU06117454a06d9c041600bf07075952fc5ee49.pdf).</details> | No change |
| Kenya | The state micro-loan fund has [disbursed Sh90 billion to 28 million registered borrowers](https://www.the-star.co.ke/news/2026-08-18-ruto-at-4-hustler-fund-has-it-built-businesses-or-mainly-provided-credit), against state funding down from KSh 20bn to KSh 300m. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Sh76 billion has been repaid with Sh13.7 billion outstanding, Sh7 billion saved by borrowers and 10 million repeat borrowers; the figures are the fund's chief executive and the president's own, and the claim that 4.5 million customers moved to better credit scores is not independently held ([four-year account](https://www.the-star.co.ke/news/2026-08-18-ruto-at-4-hustler-fund-has-it-built-businesses-or-mainly-provided-credit)).<br><br>Default runs at 15 per cent, about KSh 12.5bn, and the Auditor-General found 104,631 loans worth KSh 116.5m issued to borrowers with no identity details on file ([default and audit](https://www.the-star.co.ke/news/2026-07-23-borrowers-can-now-repay-hustler-fund-loans-using-bonga-points)).<br><br>Repayment through a privately issued loyalty balance went live in July 2026, five points settling one shilling, recovering KSh 3m in the first week — a private loyalty currency converting into public-money recovery at a rate the issuer sets ([loyalty repayment](https://www.the-star.co.ke/news/2026-07-23-borrowers-can-now-repay-hustler-fund-loans-using-bonga-points)).<br><br>The Treasury extended the payroll database, in place since 1998, to pensioners from the end of August 2025 so that [salaries and pensions are disbursed through one system](https://www.businessdailyafrica.com/bd/economy/treasury-shifts-salary-pension-payments-to-new-online-system-5152690), which the cabinet secretary put as a measure against ghost workers, duplicates and erroneous payments. No pensioner count moved, duplicate removed or saving realised is published.</details> | Regressed |

| Liberia | Government-to-person mobile payments have routed [exclusively through the national switch](https://cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/NEPS-IIPS%20Directive%20-%20092325.pdf) since 27 October 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [directive](https://cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/NEPS-IIPS%20Directive%20-%20092325.pdf) names salaries, pensions, social benefits and other transfers, with settlement into the central account. The [governor's launch remarks](https://cbl.org.lr/index.php/media/features-articles/special-remark-executive-governor-henry-f-saamoi-launch-iips-december-16) of 16 December 2025 set out what the instant payment platform is to carry, including government-to-person payments, as the first phase of the national switch.<br><br>The largest live government-to-person flow runs outside it on an operator rail: the social protection project reports [over 15,000 households paid and more than L$1 billion disbursed](https://realise.liberiasp.gov.lr/2026/01/27/president-boakai-hails-social-protection-interventions-through-realise-project/), delivered through mobile money, against a scheduled project closure in late 2026 with no successor named.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | [Ratibak Lahzi](https://www.lananews.com/ar/?p=498421) carried July 2026 salaries for 1.7m of 2.2m public employees, 76 per cent, up from 72 per cent in March. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The real-time salary system launched in August 2025 with about 2m records collected, the eastern government refusing to share employee banking data ([launch](https://almenassa.ly/en/2025/08/11/libyas-real-time-salary-system-sparks-debate-over-transparency-control-and-political-legitimacy)).<br><br>Enrolment reached 72 per cent in March 2026 ([March](https://cbl.gov.ly/%d9%85%d8%b5%d8%b1%d9%81-%d9%84%d9%8a%d8%a8%d9%8a%d8%a7-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%85%d8%b1%d9%83%d8%b2%d9%8a-%d9%8a%d8%b9%d9%84%d9%86-%d8%a7%d8%b3%d8%aa%d9%84%d8%a7%d9%85-%d8%b1%d9%88%d8%a7%d8%aa%d8%a8-%d8%b4-5/)), 75 per cent in June ([June](https://cbl.gov.ly/%d9%8a%d9%8f%d8%b9%d9%84%d9%86-%d9%85%d8%b5%d8%b1%d9%81-%d9%84%d9%8a%d8%a8%d9%8a%d8%a7-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%85%d8%b1%d9%83%d8%b2%d9%8a-%d8%a8%d8%af%d8%a1-%d8%b5%d8%b1%d9%81-%d9%85%d8%b1%d8%aa%d8%a8%d8%a7-2/)) and 76 per cent in July, when July salaries were executed through it ([July](https://www.lananews.com/ar/?p=498421), [confirmation](https://cbl.gov.ly/%d9%8a%d9%8f%d8%b9%d9%84%d9%86-%d9%85%d8%b5%d8%b1%d9%81-%d9%84%d9%8a%d8%a8%d9%8a%d8%a7-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%85%d8%b1%d9%83%d8%b2%d9%8a-%d8%a8%d8%af%d8%a1-%d8%b5%d8%b1%d9%81-%d9%85%d8%b1%d8%aa%d8%a8%d8%a7-3/)).<br><br>About 500,000 public employees remain outside it, with no published breakdown of who they are ([remainder](https://www.lananews.com/ar/?p=498421)).</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | The [government payment card framework was abrogated](https://www.lexpress.mg/2026/04/paiement-numerique-le-decret-sur-la.html) in April 2026 after technical failures and payment delays. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A card to digitalise pension payment was adopted by decree in April 2022 ([adoption](http://mef.gov.mg/page_personnalisee/index/news/1010)).<br><br>The audit court found 76,073 of 250,000 cards distributed and unused, with the scheme administratively suspended from July 2024 ([audit](https://ccomptes.mg/uploads/AUDIT-DU-MNDPT1774881345.pdf)); the framework decree was abrogated in Council of Ministers in April 2026, with over 110,000 cards reported in circulation among students ([abrogation](https://www.lexpress.mg/2026/04/paiement-numerique-le-decret-sur-la.html)).<br><br>A proposed USD 50 million additional financing would fund transition of cash transfers to mobile payments in partnership with mobile operators ([financing](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099012326100017065/txt/P179466-b0e3c23f-4024-4dae-9418-88897afe4e50.txt)).</details> | Regressed |
| Malawi | A [mobile-money operator paid about 6 billion kwacha to more than 27,760 social cash transfer beneficiaries in Mangochi](https://www.malawivoice.com/2026/05/01/gender-ministry-hails-tnm-mpamba-for-transforming-social-cash-transfers-in-mangochi/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The pipeline is documented end to end. The government's urban cash intervention evaluation [records transfers reaching beneficiaries through both mobile-money operators, with the beneficiary registry and cash transfer payroll behind them and cross-checking against the identity and mobile-money databases](https://finance.gov.mw/documents/uploads/2025-02/Revised%20Final%20CUCI%20Report.pdf).<br><br>2026-05 - one operator [disbursed roughly 6 billion kwacha in social cash transfers to more than 27,760 beneficiaries in Mangochi](https://www.malawivoice.com/2026/05/01/gender-ministry-hails-tnm-mpamba-for-transforming-social-cash-transfers-in-mangochi/), which is the only district-level figure the base holds.<br><br>2025-12 - the government's fiscal policy statement [commits to digitising all social-protection payments across all 28 districts to cut fraud and leakage](https://www.finance.gov.mw/documents/uploads/2025-12/2026%20EFPS%20FINAL-PRINT%20VERSION.pdf), with no completion date. One district measured and twenty-eight promised is the shape of the record.</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | A [bank-channel pilot for pension payment](https://maliemergenceinfo.com/2026/07/28/%f0%9d%90%8ba-digitalisation-des-services-de-la-sante-et-du-developpement-social-l%f0%9d%90%9a-%f0%9d%90%aa%f0%9d%90%ae%f0%9d%90%9a%f0%9d%90%a5%f0%9d%90%a2%f0%9d%90%ad%f0%9d%90%9e-%f0%9d%90%9d/) completed in July 2026 and is reported ready for phased extension to other partner banks. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Mobile pension payment was already live; the bank-channel pilot with one partner bank completed on 27 July 2026 and is reported ready for phased extension ([pilot](https://maliemergenceinfo.com/2026/07/28/%f0%9d%90%8ba-digitalisation-des-services-de-la-sante-et-du-developpement-social-l%f0%9d%90%9a-%f0%9d%90%aa%f0%9d%90%ae%f0%9d%90%9a%f0%9d%90%a5%f0%9d%90%a2%f0%9d%90%ad%f0%9d%90%9e-%f0%9d%90%9d/)).<br><br>No pensioner count, payment volume or extension date is published. It is the only government-to-person payment stream recorded for the country.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | A [social contribution income allowance](https://www.mra.mu/download/FAQs-SocialContributionIncomeAllowance.pdf) is administered by the revenue authority, with eligibility and payment terms published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The revenue authority publishes the allowance's terms in a frequently-asked-questions document of January 2026 ([document](https://www.mra.mu/download/FAQs-SocialContributionIncomeAllowance.pdf)).<br><br>The 2026-2030 fintech strategy sets out the payment rails such transfers run over ([strategy](https://financialservices.govmu.org/Documents/National%20Fintech%20Strategy%20Report%20%28Digital%29-1.pdf)).<br><br>No payment volume, disbursement channel breakdown or coverage rate is published for the allowance, so the base can say the mechanism exists and not how much of the population it reaches or by what route.</details> | No change, a standing allowance with no earlier position held |

It is a press figure rather than a programme report, and no payment volume or transfer value through the registries is held.</details> | Advanced, on a press figure |
| Mozambique | More than [US$14.4m has been paid to nearly 246,000 health workers](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/11/who-mozambique-channels-14-4m-through-mobile-money-for-health-campaigns/) by mobile money since 2024, replacing cash for vaccination and emergency campaigns. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The cumulative figure to August 2026 is more than US$14.4m paid to nearly 246,000 health workers and volunteers, replacing cash and giving funders a full digital transaction record ([payments](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/11/who-mozambique-channels-14-4m-through-mobile-money-for-health-campaigns/)).<br><br>The figures are the agency's own, the payments run through commercial mobile-money operators the account does not name, and no per-campaign breakdown or failure rate is published. It is the country's only recorded government-to-person digital payment stream, and it is run by an international agency rather than by the state.</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | The [first live government-to-person payments ran on 17 June 2026](https://technews.com.na/2026/06/17/namibia-moves-government-grant-payments-onto-instant-payment-network/) to marginalised-beneficiary grant recipients, through [one certified bank](https://technews.com.na/2026/06/17/namibia-moves-government-grant-payments-onto-instant-payment-network/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The first live government-to-person payments were made on 17 June 2026 to beneficiaries in the marginalised category, facilitated by one of the first banks certified under the instant payment programme ([payments](https://technews.com.na/2026/06/17/namibia-moves-government-grant-payments-onto-instant-payment-network/)).<br><br>Government social grants are phase one of the platform's rollout; the operator is a subsidiary of the central bank working with an Indian payments partner, runs strictly as a payment system operator and does not serve consumers directly, and the central bank's stated plan is to withdraw from operations and hand the platform to the industry once its public-interest goals are met ([fact sheet](https://www.marketwatch.com.na/market-watch/fact-sheet-namibias-instant-payment-solution-ips-nmh012158-3015-14760)).<br><br>Biometric arrangements for the system were being weighed a year before it went live ([biometrics](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202507/namibia-weighs-biometrics-options-for-foundation-of-instant-payment-system)). No grant volume, beneficiary count or disbursed value through the platform is published, so the base records the channel and not what has passed through it ([launch](https://thebrief.com.na/2026/07/namibia-becomes-fourth-african-country-to-launch-instant-payments-system/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Niger | [Scholarship payment through the Treasury application](https://anp.ne/niger-plusieurs-reformes-engagees-pour-ameliorer-le-secteur-de-lenseignement-superieur-et-les-performances-de-lanab/) was announced in July 2026 as part of a grants agency reform, with no budget, vendor or beneficiary count published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It is stated as an intention with no budget, vendor, timetable or beneficiary count published ([scholarships](https://anp.ne/niger-plusieurs-reformes-engagees-pour-ameliorer-le-secteur-de-lenseignement-superieur-et-les-performances-de-lanab/)).<br><br>It is the only government-to-person digital payment stream named for the country, and it is an announcement rather than a channel.</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | The [central bank digital currency whose guidelines defined government-to-person transaction types](https://www.cbn.gov.ng/Out/2021/FPRD/eNairaCircularAndGuidelines%20FINAL.pdf) is [being redesigned toward a wholesale model](https://thenationonlineng.net/payment-vision-2028-is-nigeria-ready-for-digital-economy/), removing the retail channel. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [2021 regulatory guidelines established the currency under the central bank Act and the banking Act and explicitly defined person-to-government and government-to-person transactions, and transactions from ministries, departments and agencies to persons](https://www.cbn.gov.ng/Out/2021/FPRD/eNairaCircularAndGuidelines%20FINAL.pdf) — the primary legal basis the base holds for government-to-person payment capability on that rail.<br><br>The currency is now [being redesigned toward a wholesale model](https://thenationonlineng.net/payment-vision-2028-is-nigeria-ready-for-digital-economy/), which withdraws the retail leg those provisions describe. No replacement retail government-to-person rail is named.</details> | Regressed |
| Rwanda | Social transfer recipients [now draw payments through mobile money agents](https://www.loda.gov.rw/updates/news-detail/loda-launches-awareness-campaign-to-help-vup-recipients-access-support-through-digital-payments) rather than travelling to a savings cooperative. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A campaign launched on 30 August 2025, running in three districts from 19 August, requires Vision Umurenge Programme recipients to link their Umurenge savings and credit cooperative account to a subscriber line registered against their national identity; transfers then arrive with an SMS notification and are drawn at nearby mobile money agents ([campaign](https://www.loda.gov.rw/updates/news-detail/loda-launches-awareness-campaign-to-help-vup-recipients-access-support-through-digital-payments)).<br><br>Every Umurenge cooperative has been digitised and ten districts have consolidated theirs into district cooperatives running mobile banking, but the agreements between those cooperatives and the telecom operators that would allow direct transfer are unsigned ([campaign](https://www.loda.gov.rw/updates/news-detail/loda-launches-awareness-campaign-to-help-vup-recipients-access-support-through-digital-payments)).<br><br>The account is the local development agency's own. It gives no count of recipients enrolled, no transfer volume, and no charge borne by the recipient at the agent.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | A [USD 35 million crisis-response grant](https://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/099052226161011764/pdf/BOSIB-f5748215-078d-4c4f-8320-813dab960995.pdf) scales drought-response digital and mobile cash transfers through the national social protection delivery system. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The project paper approving the additional financing treats the social protection delivery system and unified social registry as the operative national channel for government-to-person payment, scaling drought-response transfers through them ([paper](https://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/099052226161011764/pdf/BOSIB-f5748215-078d-4c4f-8320-813dab960995.pdf)).<br><br>The underlying operation, approved at USD 112 million in September 2025, is expected to reach over 738,000 people and includes a national socio-economic survey covering 12 million ([approval](https://www.worldbank.org/en/news/press-release/2025/10/28/over-738000-somalis-to-benefit-from-world-bank-supported-initiative-for-jobs-social-protection-and-climate-resilience)).<br><br>The instant payment layer added in January 2025 carries government-to-person transactions across banks, wallets and terminals ([description](https://centralbank.gov.so/nps/)).</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | Grant card migration reached [172,146 of 463,836 replaced by 28 July 2026](https://pmg.org.za/committee-meeting/43437/), and a state payment credential and wallet [entered active development with testing dated November 2026](https://www.treasury.gov.za/comm_media/press/2026/Operation%20Vulindlela%20Progress%20Report%20Q1%20-%202026.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07/08 — of [463,836 active gold cards at 29 April 2026, 172,146 had been replaced by 28 July and 264,263 remained](https://pmg.org.za/committee-meeting/43437/), roughly 40% of them in two provinces, against a deadline of [31 August 2026](https://iol.co.za/thepost/news/2026-07-24-deadline-looms-your-sassa-gold-cards-will-no-longer-work-after-august-31/). The bank reports 306 replacement sites against a target of 300 plus nine mobile teams, ring-fences customers more than 30km from a site, and plans the rollout from monthly data on where each customer last transacted.<br><br>2026-07-31 — the state's own rail moved: the payments modernisation programme [passed from conceptual design into active development of the payment credential, wallet, governance and trust capabilities, with testing against government use cases dated November 2026](https://www.treasury.gov.za/comm_media/press/2026/Operation%20Vulindlela%20Progress%20Report%20Q1%20-%202026.pdf).<br><br>The base holds no statement of how the state credential is meant to relate to the bank-owned rails it would sit beside, which is the question a G2P wallet raises before it raises any other.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | A closed project's rails [reached 8.7 million people](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099835506232634636/pdf/IDU-3ba3fb18-dbd6-4de0-92c5-7b0d80492366.pdf); a successor safety net made a [first payment to 172,388](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099091925103035254/txt/P505963-29e6468c-9705-464a-a172-383e7b1bf9e4.txt) in August 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The retrospective on the earlier programme records a unified registration platform and payment infrastructure reaching 8.7 million people, built in collaboration with the national civil registry and suspended after the 2021 coup, with its registries and rails reported still usable by successor programmes ([retrospective](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099835506232634636/pdf/IDU-3ba3fb18-dbd6-4de0-92c5-7b0d80492366.pdf)).<br><br>The successor's implementation report of September 2025 records targeting, identification and registration completed in five localities and a first cash payment reaching 172,388 people, over a quarter of a 602,000 caseload target, with biometric registration tracked as a management indicator ([report](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099091925103035254/txt/P505963-29e6468c-9705-464a-a172-383e7b1bf9e4.txt)).</details> | Advanced |
| Tanzania | [Digital crop payments to farmers](https://dailynews.co.tz/mixx-digital-payments-boost-farmers-incomes-businesses/) were reported by season at a national agricultural exhibition, all figures the operator's own. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The figures were given at a promotional exhibition at which the operator also awarded vehicles and phones to loyal users; no farmer total, fee schedule or default rate on the linked credit is published ([payments](https://dailynews.co.tz/mixx-digital-payments-boost-farmers-incomes-businesses/), [northern](https://www.thecitizen.co.tz/tanzania/news/national/over-2-000-northern-tanzania-farmers-benefit-from-mixx-digital-services-5551992), [southern](https://www.thecitizen.co.tz/tanzania/news/national/mixx-by-yas-eases-payments-for-southern-highlands-farmers-5552008)).<br><br>Crop payment digitisation is the largest routine government-to-person flow in an agricultural economy, and the only account of it comes from the company that carries it.</details> | Advanced |
| Togo | [Retirement allowances began digital disbursement](https://actu-togo.tg/2026/08/04/togo-paiements-des-allocations-de-retraite-les-beneficiaires-invites-a-utiliser-le-numero-vert-8220/) in July 2026 with registration of payment details required by 31 August, and no alternative is stated for a beneficiary without a bank account, a handset or an identity document. <details><summary>Full record</summary>No beneficiary count, disbursed value or registration rate is published ([allowances](https://actu-togo.tg/2026/08/04/togo-paiements-des-allocations-de-retraite-les-beneficiaires-invites-a-utiliser-le-numero-vert-8220/)).<br><br>A treasury interoperable prepaid card was announced with no launch timetable; two regional precedents exist but the central bank's own register counted only two treasury issuers at end-2024, which a three-state account does not match ([card](https://www.togofirst.com/en/public-management/2407-19673-togo-plans-prepaid-cards-to-digitize-public-payments)).<br><br>The regional guide to electronic-money issuance by public treasuries settles closed-loop issuance, a dedicated escrow for holders' funds and a complaints route, requires tariff disclosure and sets no fee cap ([guide](https://www.bceao.int/sites/default/files/2021-04/GUIDE%20POUR%20LA%20CONSTITUTION%20DES%20DOSSIERS%20POUR%20L%E2%80%99E%CC%81MISSION%20DE%20LA%20MONNAIE%20E%CC%81LECTRONIQUE%20PAR%20LES%20TRE%CC%81SORS%20PUBLICS%20NATIONAUX.pdf)). A hard registration deadline with no stated fallback is the access question this unit does not answer.</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | The [social cash transfer](https://www.zambiamonitor.com/govt-claims-k1-4-billion-to-be-spent-in-july-august-under-social-cash-transfer-programme/) began its July-August 2026 cycle disbursement, on the ministry's own figures with no redemption rate, provider list or exclusion figure published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The cycle disbursement began in August 2026 ([disbursement](https://www.zambiamonitor.com/govt-claims-k1-4-billion-to-be-spent-in-july-august-under-social-cash-transfer-programme/)).<br><br>A provincial minister separately names four districts where poor network service blocks access to social cash transfers and farm input subsidies, so the exclusion figure nobody publishes is the one that would measure whether the transfer arrives.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | An integrated [payroll and pensions system](https://www.psc.gov.zw/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/The-Pay-Master-Speaks-GovPay-2026-Newsflash-.pdf) is operational in over 60 ministries, and the social security authority pays pensions into mobile money wallets. <details><summary>Full record</summary>GovPay replaces a payroll platform of thirty-two years and covers every government employee and pensioner drawing from the Consolidated Revenue Fund; electronic payslips go to government electronic mail, a portal carries payslips, leave balances, loans and service records, and the system is operational in over 60 ministries and grant-aided institutions ([newsflash](https://www.psc.gov.zw/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/The-Pay-Master-Speaks-GovPay-2026-Newsflash-.pdf)).<br><br>Its named integration partners are the revenue authority, banks, the social security authority, the government internet service provider and the telecommunications regulator, where the platform it replaces could not communicate with any of them ([newsflash](https://www.psc.gov.zw/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/The-Pay-Master-Speaks-GovPay-2026-Newsflash-.pdf)).<br><br>The state social security authority says pensions can be paid straight into a mobile money wallet ([pensions](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/national-social-security-authority-nssa_nssaheroes-convenience-digitalservices-activity-7463468848600715264-CH3I)).<br><br>No disbursement value, recipient count or take-up rate is held for either channel, and whether the full GovPay launch happened inside its stated 2026 target is not on the base.</details> | Advanced |

## Revenue collection

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | Electronic tax payment is now [mandatory for large enterprises and optional for others](https://www.echoroukonline.com/bank-cards-and-transfers-introduced-for-tax-payments), with a first user guide published in August 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Tax data was [integrated across the assessment and contribution platforms in March 2025](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/public-management/algeria-orders-fast-tracking-of-digital-tax-data-integration).<br><br>2026-08-05 - the tax directorate published its first user guide for electronic payment: it is [mandatory for taxpayers under the large-enterprise directorate and optional for others, covering only tax and duties from electronic declarations rather than existing debts; payment by transfer or card generates a verifiable electronic receipt, and a rejected transaction cannot be retried electronically, the taxpayer having to attend a local office instead](https://www.echoroukonline.com/bank-cards-and-transfers-introduced-for-tax-payments). The guide lists seventeen rejection reasons, and no volume or adoption figure is published.<br><br>The [fiscal stamp-duty payment platform has been live since August 2024](https://algeriainvest.com/premium-news/une-plateforme-numerique-pour-le-paiement-des-droits-de-timbres-fiscaux) and [carries commercial registration duties](https://techafricanews.com/2026/01/05/algerian-ministries-launch-digital-stamp-duty-payment-service-for-commercial-registration/).</details> | Advanced |
| Angola | Mandatory electronic invoicing [reached 51,245 enrolled taxpayers and 36,341,801 invoices by August 2026](https://angop.ao/en/noticias/economia/mais-de-50-mil-contribuintes-aderem-a-facturacao-electronica-em-angola/), covering large taxpayers and state suppliers. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-23 — [51,245 large taxpayers and state suppliers were enrolled and 36,341,801 invoices issued](https://angop.ao/en/noticias/economia/mais-de-50-mil-contribuintes-aderem-a-facturacao-electronica-em-angola/), against a mandate [in force from 1 January 2026](https://novojornal.co.ao/economia/detalhe/agt-comeca-a-aplicar-facturacao-electronica-em-1-de-janeiro-de-2026-68608.html) after [an earlier postponement](https://kpmg.com/us/en/taxnewsflash/news/2025/10/angola-mandatory-e-invoicing-implementation-postponed.html) and with no enrolment figure published before this one.<br><br>The figures are the tax authority's own and the mandate covers large taxpayers and state suppliers rather than all taxpayers, so this is neither universal coverage nor an independently verified count. The enforcement side is blunt and already live: [a taxpayer not invoicing electronically cannot be paid by the State](https://forbesafricalusofona.com/agt-garante-interoperabilidade-da-certidao-tributaria-nos-orgaos-do-estado-angolano/).<br><br>It is the largest transaction volume of any government digital system on this ledger, and it runs on a public key infrastructure that has no governing law, reported under authentication.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | A 1% levy on large cash payments [pushes merchants onto digital rails](https://fraternite.bj/transactions-financieres-au-benin-le-mobile-money-pour-contourner-la-taxe-sur-les-paiements-en-especes/), joined by [the 2026 rectifying law's digital taxes](https://fraternite.bj/numerique-investissements-et-controles-fiscaux-les-grands-changements-du-projet-de-loi-de-finances-rectificative/) and [cashless motorway tolls](https://fraternite.bj/siratpay-le-paiement-numerique-simplifie-les-peages-au-benin/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[A 1% levy on large cash payments has been in force since January 2025, with mobile money the route around it](https://fraternite.bj/transactions-financieres-au-benin-le-mobile-money-pour-contourner-la-taxe-sur-les-paiements-en-especes/), and 2026-06-11 — [the rectifying finance law added digital taxes](https://fraternite.bj/numerique-investissements-et-controles-fiscaux-les-grands-changements-du-projet-de-loi-de-finances-rectificative/).<br><br>2026-03-19 — [cashless motorway tolling went live](https://fraternite.bj/siratpay-le-paiement-numerique-simplifie-les-peages-au-benin/), where none existed.<br><br>Taken together these are fiscal instruments doing digital-inclusion work: the state is taxing cash and removing it from a tolled network rather than subsidising the alternative. No revenue figure, adoption rate or effect on cash use is published for either measure, and the tax directorate's own system modernisation, reported under sectoral systems, has published no delivery.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | Value added tax on non-resident digital services [took effect in June 2026 with a collection target of about P450m a year](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/botswana-expands-digital-tax-net/news), and [electronic invoicing is provided for with no start date](https://bw.andersen.com/botswanas-new-tax-rules-take-effect/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-06-01 — [a 14% value added tax on non-resident digital and remote services took effect, with a stated collection target of about P450m a year](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/botswana-expands-digital-tax-net/news), [the revenue service having set out its intent to tax global digital providers](https://www.mmegi.bw/business/burs-looks-to-tax-global-digital-services-providers/news), against [a tax system described three months earlier as built for tangible goods](https://www.sundaystandard.info/botswanas-tax-system-built-for-tangible-goods-now-faces-the-internet/).<br><br>2026-08-16 — [mandatory electronic invoicing is provided for by the 2025 value added tax statute in force from 1 July 2026, with no start date, threshold or scope published](https://bw.andersen.com/botswanas-new-tax-rules-take-effect/). The account is a tax adviser's reading of the Act; no revenue-service notice or timetable is on record.<br><br>The system that would carry both is unfunded through the estimates: [P4.13bn for revenue-service modernisation, covering electronic invoicing, digital taxation and border infrastructure, sits in a development plan with no appropriation trail](https://www.sundaystandard.info/finance-ministry-bets-p8-8bn-on-digital-discipline/).</details> | Advanced |
| Burkina Faso | A certified electronic invoice [launched in January 2026](https://www.sidwaya.info/modernisation-de-ladministration-fiscale-la-facture-electronique-certifiee-officiellement-lancee/), and the national payments platform [became the collection rail for automated traffic fines](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article148326). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-01-06 — [a certified electronic invoice entered operation, with control modules for firms already running invoicing software and invoicing units for those that do not](https://www.sidwaya.info/modernisation-de-ladministration-fiscale-la-facture-electronique-certifiee-officiellement-lancee/), replacing a 2017 paper standard as the traceability instrument. No mandatory date and no adoption figure is stated.<br><br>2026-08-01 — [the national payments platform, which already carried stamp duty, taxes and duties by application, web and short code](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/solutions/burkina-faso-launches-digital-payment-platform-to-streamline-government-services), [gained payment links for automated traffic fines, with vehicle immobilisation for non-payment within a month](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article148326).<br><br>That is the first case on file of a domestic payment rail carrying a coercive state obligation: the same channel that collects a tax now enforces a penalty, [interconnected to the vehicle database and the enforcement cameras](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/tech/burkina-faso-des-cameras-intelligentes-pour-surveiller-les-infractions-routieres-a-ouagadougou).</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | The online tax platform [took over all new taxpayer registration from 4 August 2026](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/08/06/e-kori-toutes-les-nouvelles-demandes-dimmatriculation-desormais-en-ligne-a-partir-du-4-aout/), while [invoicing machines stop with every power cut](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/08/20/les-coupures-delectricite-compliquent-la-facturation-electronique-des-commercants-a-karusi/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-01-19 — [the online tax platform launched for declaration and payment](https://www.burunditimes.com/burundi-revenue-authority-rolls-out-e-kori-digital-tax-platform/), [reported at the time as the country's first](https://techafricanews.com/2026/01/22/burundi-modernizes-tax-and-non-tax-revenue-collection-with-ekori-launch). 2026-08-04 — [all new taxpayer registration became exclusively online through it, for natural and legal persons and cooperatives, with commercial registration routed through the business agency's portal, cooperative registration through the cooperatives agency, and biometric enrolment for natural persons](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/08/06/e-kori-toutes-les-nouvelles-demandes-dimmatriculation-desormais-en-ligne-a-partir-du-4-aout/).<br><br>2025-12-25 — [the revised finance law requires banks to transfer telepaid taxes daily, with a penalty of 100% of the amount collected for missing the deadline](https://finances.gov.bi/index.php/2025/12/25/projet-de-loi-de-finances-2025-2026-revise-le-gouvernement-defend-des-choix-budgetaires-responsables-devant-le-parlement/).<br><br>Against it, 2026-08-20 — [traders in one province report that electronic invoicing machines stop working whenever the power does, halting sales and invoice issuance with every outage, with operators of weak literacy or digital skills reported to struggle](https://abpinfo.bi/2026/08/20/les-coupures-delectricite-compliquent-la-facturation-electronique-des-commercants-a-karusi/). Delivery had stood at [500 of 10,000 machines ordered](https://www.iwacu-burundi.org/revision-budgetaire-2024-2025-des-imprevus-majeurs-en-ligne-de-mire/). No national outage figure, downtime measure or offline-mode provision is published.</details> | Advanced, with the invoicing machines stopping whenever the power does |
| Cameroon | An [order of 28 May 2025 made the Treasury platform the only lawful channel for non-tax revenue](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/cameroon-mandates-digital-payment-platform-for-non-tax-revenues), and device duties moved onto the customs platform. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-05-28 - an order [made the Treasury's platform the only lawful channel for collecting non-tax revenue, giving named departments three months to use it exclusively, accepting mobile money, bank cards and post-office counter payments with receipt authentication and real-time tracking, against a 2025 non-tax revenue target of FCFA 361bn on an estimated potential of nearly FCFA 600bn](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/cameroon-mandates-digital-payment-platform-for-non-tax-revenues). Nothing reports compliance after the deadline.<br><br>2026-04-01 - [importers must declare each device by its identifier through the customs platform and pay duties before network use, with an amnesty for devices connected before that date, customs putting annual losses from undeclared devices at about US$21.5m](https://techcabal.com/2026/04/01/cameroon-imei-system-mobile-device-import-duties/). Enforcement follows: [deactivation of handsets on which duty has not been paid begins 1 September 2026 under a communique of 20 August 2026, after an earlier date of 25 May 2026 was abandoned when operators objected that no test phase had preceded a full-scale deployment](https://www.digitalbusiness.africa/blocage-des-telephones-non-dedouanes-cameroun-enfin-le-consensus-entre-les-acteurs/). A [card for fully digital settlement of customs duties was reported as about to be signed in March 2026](https://leconomie.info/commerce-exterieur-le-cameroun-se-dote-dun-outil-de-paiement-douanier-digital/), with no launch date on file.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | The online tax platform [launched in March 2025 with four modules and 301 large and 325 medium firms in scope](https://www.agenceecofin.com/actualites-numerique/2503-126985-la-centrafrique-lance-officiellement-e-tax-sa-plateforme-de-paiement-d-impots-en-ligne); no filing, payment or coverage figure has been published since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The platform launched officially after adoption and training from January 2025, with four modules including registration for the unique identification number, run by the tax directorate under a 24-month external service contract, and 301 large and 325 medium firms in scope](https://www.agenceecofin.com/actualites-numerique/2503-126985-la-centrafrique-lance-officiellement-e-tax-sa-plateforme-de-paiement-d-impots-en-ligne). Nothing on it is dated inside the window, no filing, payment or coverage figure has been published, and the 2026 estimates carry no line for it.<br><br>The fiscal case for it is now made from outside: a public finance review estimates that digitising the tax administration could raise up to two points of national output in the short term, reported under data governance. The system that would do it has been live for eighteen months and has published nothing about what it collects.<br><br>Customs is the line with money behind it, reported under domestic financing.</details> | No change |
| Chad | The [exclusive electronic state-payment regime](https://lendjampost.com/budget-2027-le-tchad-mise-sur-le-numerique-pour-verrouiller-les-finances-publiques/) slipped to a 1 January 2027 start, and [3,000 payment terminals](https://www.lepaystchad.com/tchad-3-000-terminaux-de-paiement-electronique-remis-au-ministere-des-finances-pour-moderniser-la-collecte-des-recettes-publiques/) were delivered with nothing showing one reaching a counter. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The regime was set out in the 2026 budget as zero cash and zero paper and has been restated for a 2027 start; the slip is the finding ([regime](https://lendjampost.com/budget-2027-le-tchad-mise-sur-le-numerique-pour-verrouiller-les-finances-publiques/), [budget](https://lendjampost.com/budget-2026-au-tchad-zero-cash-zero-papier-zero-facture-fantome/)).<br><br>Three thousand smart terminals were delivered to the finance ministry under the lender operation, and nothing on the record shows a terminal reaching a counter ([terminals](https://www.lepaystchad.com/tchad-3-000-terminaux-de-paiement-electronique-remis-au-ministere-des-finances-pour-moderniser-la-collecte-des-recettes-publiques/)).<br><br>A framework agreement on mobile payment of public services was worked on with four operators in February 2026 and nothing since shows it concluded ([framework](https://journaldutchad.com/tchad-le-gouvernement-accelere-lintegration-du-paiement-mobile-des-services-publics/)).</details> | Stalled |
| Comoros | [Electronic filing and payment have a statutory basis](https://finances.gouv.km/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/Decret-portant-promulgation-du-loi-des-finances-2026.pdf) and [implementing texts were validated in April 2026](https://finances.gouv.km/actualite_post/atelier-dgi-au-retaj-sur-la-validation-du-cadre-juridique-des-teleprocedures-fiscales-2026/); no activation date is set. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-12 - the finance law [inserted a section into the fiscal-procedures code establishing electronic filing and payment of taxes, duties, penalties and fees through an official platform, with a phased obligation on large and medium taxpayers, the legal value of an electronic filing, authentication, archiving and penalties for not using it, implementation dates left to regulation](https://finances.gouv.km/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/Decret-portant-promulgation-du-loi-des-finances-2026.pdf).<br><br>2026-04 - the tax directorate [ran a three-day workshop, financed by French development cooperation, to validate that legal framework ahead of a progressive rollout](https://finances.gouv.km/actualite_post/atelier-dgi-au-retaj-sur-la-validation-du-cadre-juridique-des-teleprocedures-fiscales-2026/).<br><br>2026-01 - the current fund review [names mobilising domestic revenue through tax and customs administration reform among its policy priorities](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1comea2026001-source-pdf.pdf). Nothing in the 2026 vote appropriates for the platform.</details> | Advanced |
| Congo | The [public-revenue platform went live for online payment of taxes, levies and customs duties](https://leconomie.info/congo-le-paiement-des-impots-et-taxes-bascule-en-ligne/), with all taxpayers targeted by end-2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Nothing on file names the platform before the order of May 2026; tax payment ran through a single window at the postal bank. It is now [live on the national payments switch, with payment following a declaration on the tax or customs system, authenticated, reconciled in real time and routed to the treasury against a secured electronic receipt, rolling out at large then medium enterprises with all taxpayers targeted by end-2026](https://leconomie.info/congo-le-paiement-des-impots-et-taxes-bascule-en-ligne/). Wave dates for the two cohorts are not published, and the [order makes it the sole lawful channel](https://sgg.cg/JO/2026/congo-jo-2026-24-2.pdf).<br><br>The filing front end is the tax portal, whose [user guide fixes filing regimes, delegation and irreversible submission, one delegate at a time with the legal representative's rights removed for the duration and no correction route stated](https://www.finances.gouv.cg/fr/download/file/fid/10535).<br><br>2026-07-15 - a [steering committee chaired by the finance minister and seating the directors-general of the revenue authorities held a first meeting on three objectives: improving revenue collection, securing public funds and raising the quality of public spending](https://www.finances.gouv.cg/fr/articles/ouverture-du-copil-de-la-transformation-num%C3%A9rique-de-la-gestion-des-finances-publiques).</details> | Advanced |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [tax portal carried more than 78% of the tax directorate's collected revenue in 2021](https://apif.finances.gouv.ci/index.php?catid=5&id=214&Itemid=230&option=com_content&view=article), with nothing published since. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The portal was opened by the tax directorate in January 2017. As at 15 March 2022 it had [more than 74,000 registered taxpayers, and carried over 22% of the directorate's collected revenue in its first year and more than 78% in 2021](https://apif.finances.gouv.ci/index.php?catid=5&id=214&Itemid=230&option=com_content&view=article).<br><br>No figure is on file since March 2022, so nothing in the window moves this indicator: what is stated is a four-year-old position on a system that is plainly still central to revenue collection. A current registration or revenue-share figure from the tax directorate would settle it.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | [Electronic customs clearance and payment are now obligatory](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-de-finances-n203-an-25-9eme-l-portant-budget-initial-de-letat-pour-lexercice-2026/) while the [tax system collects nothing](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099012726115520907/pdf/P180696-28ecf2f4-34e9-4559-9695-f14bd83f8798.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>On the customs side the [2026 budget law constitutes the single window by statute, makes the electronic route obligatory for all economic operators with paper admitted only on a duly established technical impossibility, requires duties, taxes and fees to be paid electronically and requires the customs and treasury systems to be interconnected for daily and monthly reconciliation](https://www.journalofficiel.dj/texte-juridique/loi-de-finances-n203-an-25-9eme-l-portant-budget-initial-de-letat-pour-lexercice-2026/).<br><br>On the tax side the [share of returns filed and paid through the integrated system stood at 0% in January 2026 against a 70% target for 2029](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099012726115520907/pdf/P180696-28ecf2f4-34e9-4559-9695-f14bd83f8798.pdf).<br><br>Between them the fund's consultation records [electronic payment introduced for vehicle road tax, stamp duty and licensing fees, short of an operational tax system](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2025/english/1djiea2025001-source-pdf.pdf).</details> | Mixed, customs collection made obligatory while the tax system stays non-operational |
| DR Congo | Standardised electronic invoicing is [mandatory since 1 December 2025, with quarterly VAT mobilisation up from CFC 280-290bn to CFC 340bn](https://actualite.cd/2026/08/06/facture-normalisee-doudou-fwamba-clot-les-cliniques-fiscales-et-annonce-des-sanctions). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system was initiated in 2017 and operationalised on 1 August 2025. It is [mandatory since 1 December 2025 for all firms liable to VAT after a two-month moratorium and a further 30-day grace period, the state having distributed more than 4,000 free billing units and electronic fiscal devices to liable small and medium firms, with quarterly VAT mobilisation risen from a CFC 280-290bn average to CFC 340bn, which the finance minister still calls very largely below potential](https://actualite.cd/2026/08/06/facture-normalisee-doudou-fwamba-clot-les-cliniques-fiscales-et-annonce-des-sanctions). Fiscal clinics closed in August 2026 with immediate targeted sanctions ordered against non-compliant taxpayers and revenue staff alike. The figures are the minister's own and unaudited.<br><br>2026-06-11 - a province [made electronic payment of an airport levy official from July through a private technical partner with real-time tracking](https://rdc-monde.com/aeroport-de-ndjili-le-paiement-electronique-du-go-pass-annonce-des-juillet/); whether it went live is not established.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | A [second facilitation package added a premium centre and a real-estate transactions app](https://sis.gov.eg/en/media-center/news/egypt-launches-new-tax-services-center-real-estate-transactions-app-as-part-of-tax-relief-drive/), against [named defects still on the record](https://www.alborsaanews.com/2026/07/29/1982823). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The tax system has run since January 2021 across returns, electronic invoices and receipts, registration, payments, automated audit, refunds, exemptions, appeals and enforced collection. 2026-08-19 - a [second facilitation package added a premium services centre and a real-estate transactions app on which citizens register transaction details, upload documents, pay taxes and obtain electronic clearance certificates online, with telephone guidance for less confident users](https://sis.gov.eg/en/media-center/news/egypt-launches-new-tax-services-center-real-estate-transactions-app-as-part-of-tax-relief-drive/); no uptake figure is published.<br><br>The defects are named by practitioners: [historic credit balances never migrated so companies holding credits are shown as debtors; audit offices demanding paper copies of documents already on the authority's databases; files moved to appeal committees on timer expiry; some payments unevidenceable, creating phantom debts; and government bodies not always issuing electronic invoices while the private sector is compelled to](https://www.alborsaanews.com/2026/07/29/1982823).<br><br>The [2018 transfer-pricing guide is unrevised, with appeal committees relying on the statutory requirement for a minister-approved guide and some penalties cancelled as a result](https://www.alborsaanews.com/2026/07/29/1982823), and a [proposed cut of the VAT credit-refund period awaits its legislative steps](https://www.alborsaanews.com/2026/07/29/1982823).</details> | Mixed, a second facilitation package added a services centre and a real-estate app while named defects in the tax system remain on the record and the 2018 transfer-pricing guide is unrevised |
| Equatorial Guinea | A [treasury single account](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_gobierno_impulsa_la_implementacion_de_la_cuenta_unica_del_tesoro) has a five-phase roadmap adopted and production targeted for the first quarter of 2027, with the annual tax licence already filed online. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A census of all state bank accounts is complete and the account is to be built on the regional central bank's own systems, with no cost, agency or supplier stated ([roadmap](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/el_gobierno_impulsa_la_implementacion_de_la_cuenta_unica_del_tesoro)).<br><br>The single tax licence moved online in January 2026, with no transaction or take-up figure published ([tax licence](https://realequatorialguinea.com/destacado/politica/el-gobierno-de-guinea-ecuatorial-avanza-en-digitalizacion-de-la-patente-tributaria-unica-anual/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Eswatini | An [electronic invoicing programme](https://times.co.sz/33269/business/ers-turns-to-technology-amid-e4-25bn-annual-tax-gap/) launched in May 2026 against an estimated E4.259 billion annual tax gap, and customs payments now clear in near real time. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The programme was launched on 11 May 2026 with a Serbian technology vendor engaged to build it, presented as the answer to an estimated E4.259 billion annual leakage ([launch](https://times.co.sz/33269/business/ers-turns-to-technology-amid-e4-25bn-annual-tax-gap/)), and rollout began with wholesale and retail businesses ([rollout](https://eswatinipositivenews.online/retailers-wholesalers-to-lead-eswatinis-new-e-invoicing-tax-system-rollout/)).<br><br>The revenue service and a commercial bank integrated their payment systems in June 2026 so customs payments reflect on the trade platform in near real time, replacing manual bank-statement reconciliation ([integration](https://independentnews.co.sz/44878/business/ers-fnb-complete-real-time-customs-payment-integration/)).<br><br>An integrated revenue administration system has been running since March 2024 ([budget speech](https://parliament.gov.sz/media/speeches/budget/2024.pdf)).<br><br>No enrolled-business count and no revenue effect is published for the invoicing programme, so the gap it was built to close cannot yet be measured against it.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | A mobile wallet began [collecting regional tax](https://techafricanews.com/2026/07/28/m-pesa-ethiopia-launches-digital-tax-payment-service-for-amhara-region/) in July 2026, described as the first of a planned series with regional revenue authorities. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The service went live with one regional revenue authority in July 2026 ([regional tax payment](https://techafricanews.com/2026/07/28/m-pesa-ethiopia-launches-digital-tax-payment-service-for-amhara-region/)).<br><br>No collection volume, taxpayer count or fee arrangement is published, and the base holds nothing on federal revenue collected through digital channels, which is the larger question.</details> | Advanced |
| Gabon | At least [80 per cent of tax revenue](https://www.ecofinagency.com/news-digital/1509-48682-gabon-targets-13-billion-2026-budget-with-revenue-digitalization-push) is collected through the revenue platform, and an online declaration portal went live in 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The tax authority's 2024 report puts at least 80 per cent of revenue through the system, live since April 2024, and no later share has been published ([revenue platform](https://www.ecofinagency.com/news-digital/1509-48682-gabon-targets-13-billion-2026-budget-with-revenue-digitalization-push)).<br><br>An online declaration and payment portal was announced live by the authority in August 2025 ([portal](http://gouvernement.ga/2025/08/18/etax-direction-generale-des-impots)).<br><br>The share is self-reported by the authority, and the base holds no electronic invoicing mandate and no national platform for the payment and collection of public revenue beyond the tax system.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | A [wallet was authorised to collect all government-service payments](https://standard.gm/govt-payments-now-digitalised-live-on-yonna-wallet-app) in March 2026, and a second platform was wired into revenue collection in July. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The first collects identity card, passport and business-registration fees with diaspora payment and auto-generated receipts; no volume or fee structure is published ([authorisation](https://standard.gm/govt-payments-now-digitalised-live-on-yonna-wallet-app)).<br><br>A memorandum with the Accountant General wires a domestically built platform, distributed through the postal service, into government revenue collection, with no go-live date, volume, fee structure or list of connected banks published and no text of the memorandum held ([memorandum](https://thepoint.gm/africa/gambia/headlines/accountant-general-seals-pact-with-sutura-to-strengthen-digital-revenue-collection)).<br><br>Two collection channels authorised in five months, neither with a published volume, is the position.</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | Electronic invoicing [took effect with the 2025 value-added tax Act](https://gra.gov.gh/e-services/e-vat/), on a [levy system connecting issuers, providers and banks](https://gra.gov.gh/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/elevy_administrative_guidelines.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The standing instrument for digital revenue collection requires [mobile-money issuers, payment-service providers and banks to connect transactions to the levy system for real-time calculation, capture, reconciliation and remittance to the government account](https://gra.gov.gh/wp-content/uploads/2023/02/elevy_administrative_guidelines.pdf).<br><br>2026-01-01 - electronic value-added tax invoicing took effect with the new Act: [free or integrated invoicing installed by the revenue authority for registered businesses, generating signed electronic receipts verifiable by code](https://gra.gov.gh/e-services/e-vat/).<br><br>2026-06 - the tax administration system's pilot had [more than 400 taxpayers onboarded at one office, with an August 2026 target for full migration and a second phase to integrate the companies registrar, the social security trust and the identification authority](https://graphiconline.com/news/general-news/ghana-news-gra-activates-new-tax-administration-platform-over-400-taxpayers-onboarded.html). No revenue attributed to any of the three is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea | A [public-payments gateway](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/finance/guinea-rolls-out-tresor-pay-as-digital-gateway-for-public-payments) launched in December 2025 for state administrative fees by mobile, alongside social contributions at about 60 per cent online. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The gateway entered service on 22 December 2025 ([gateway](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/finance/guinea-rolls-out-tresor-pay-as-digital-gateway-for-public-payments)).<br><br>The social-security platform carries about 60 per cent of contribution receipts ([contributions](https://mediaguinee.com/2025/07/modernisation-de-la-gestion-des-cotisations-sociales-la-cnss-lance-la-plateforme-ecnss/)).<br><br>No collection volume, value or fee arrangement is published for either, so the share of state revenue collected digitally is unmeasured.</details> | Advanced |
| Guinea-Bissau | The payment platform became the [sole channel for the tax identification number](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/instructions) and for [value added tax on customs clearance](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/api/public_files/LX4zIpsBZ7DXb_HFUy3A.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Two orders in December 2025 made the simplified payment platform compulsory rather than optional: the revenue directorate [banned the older financial system and made the platform the sole channel for issuing the tax identification number](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/instructions), and [mandated platform-only declaration and payment of value added tax for customs clearance, effective immediately](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/api/public_files/LX4zIpsBZ7DXb_HFUy3A.pdf).<br><br>Staff were prepared for it: [tax officials in fiscal districts and regional offices were trained ahead of the September 2025 value added tax rollout](https://ang.gw/dgci-capacita-tecnicos-de-bairros-fiscais-e-das-reparticoes-regionais-em-materia-de-iva/). The fund's [eleventh review names the platform and a taxpayer census among reform actions](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1gnbea2026002.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | [Electronic procurement carries more than 1,500 entities](https://www.kenyanews.go.ke/new-procurement-platform-to-save-sh-86-billion-in-wastage/) against a projected Sh86 billion of savings, and electronic invoicing became comprehensive in January 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform ties treasury disbursement to procurements actually made and carries budgeting and procurement plans together; the savings figure is a government projection from full uptake rather than a measured outturn, and no transaction volume is published ([procurement](https://www.kenyanews.go.ke/new-procurement-platform-to-save-sh-86-billion-in-wastage/), [appropriation](https://www.treasury.go.ke/sites/default/files/Budget%20Books/Supplementary%20I%202024-2025/Development-Supplementary-I-1011-1083.pdf)).<br><br>Electronic invoicing moved to comprehensive income and expenses validation across the entire commercial sector from 1 January 2026 — the opposite approach to the phased sector-by-sector rollouts two neighbours adopted, so three regional revenue administrations run unharmonised regimes; the underlying instruments are not held here and no compliance or coverage figures are stated ([invoicing](https://horneconomicreview.com/2026/08/19/uganda-adopts-phased-electronic-invoicing-rollout-contrasting-kenyas-simultaneous-approach/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Lesotho | [Tax e-payment integration](https://www.lena.gov.ls/rsl-slb-launch-e-payment-integration) went live in February 2026 for company tax, pay-as-you-earn, value-added tax and customs duties. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Revenue Services Lesotho and Standard Lesotho Bank launched the integration on 11 February 2026, reachable over USSD, internet banking and the bank's mobile application ([launch](https://www.lena.gov.ls/rsl-slb-launch-e-payment-integration)).<br><br>No collection figure, share of receipts taken electronically, or extension to a second bank has been published since.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | A [mobile-money tax platform](https://revenue.lra.gov.lr/no-more-standing-in-lines-to-pay-taxes-lra-orange-liberia-ecobank-launch-new-platform/) carries taxes and fees for 18 ministries over USSD without internet. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [platform launched in July 2025](https://revenue.lra.gov.lr/no-more-standing-in-lines-to-pay-taxes-lra-orange-liberia-ecobank-launch-new-platform/) connects directly to the integrated tax administration system and carries taxes and fees for 18 ministries and agencies over USSD with a text-message receipt, so it works without a data connection. It predates the central bank's routing mandate.<br><br>From [1 December 2025 all revenue collections by mobile money](https://cbl.org.lr/sites/default/files/documents/NEPS-IIPS%20Directive%20-%20092325.pdf) must route through the national switch. A [compliance audit covering 2018 to 2024](https://mfdp.gov.lr/index.php/media-center/press-release/joint-press-conference-by-the-ministry-of-finance-and-development-planning-liberia-revenue-authority-and-central-bank-of-liberia-on-gac-s-compliance-audit-report-on-the-government-of-liberia-revenue-collection-and-reconciliation-processes-for-the-period-july-1-2018-to-december-31-2024) found variances between bills and payments in the customs and tax systems, discrepancies between the tax system and the general revenue account, and unauthorised withdrawals in transitory accounts.</details> | Advanced |
| Libya | State treasuries and collecting agencies must accept electronic payment from [March 2026](https://libyaherald.com/2026/03/aldabaiba-government-and-cbl-take-further-actions-to-encourage-increased-e-payments-use-cbl-reduces-new-fx-bureaux-commission), the first duty of its kind placed on revenue bodies. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The obligation reaches treasuries and collecting agencies and was issued together with a cut in the foreign-exchange levy ([mandate](https://libyaherald.com/2026/03/aldabaiba-government-and-cbl-take-further-actions-to-encourage-increased-e-payments-use-cbl-reduces-new-fx-bureaux-commission)).<br><br>No collection figure, adoption rate or list of complying agencies has been published since, so the base holds the requirement and no measure of what it has changed.</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | Two collection systems entered service in the window: [non-tax revenue by mobile money](http://central.mef.gov.mg/page_personnalisee/index/news/1642) and an integrated tax administration platform. <details><summary>Full record</summary>A non-tax revenue system launched in May 2025 enabling 24-hour mobile-money payment of government revenues through the three mobile money operators, with progressive nationwide deployment ([launch](http://central.mef.gov.mg/page_personnalisee/index/news/1642)).<br><br>The integrated tax administration system was confirmed tested and ready for production from the end of September 2025, consolidating electronic filing, diversified payment and traceability ([production](https://new.moov.mg/actualite/106644-modernisation-fiscale-la-plateforme-safi-prete-pour-la-mise-en-production)).<br><br>Mandatory electronic invoicing was made law by decree of 2 July 2025, running through a central platform connected by interface to homologated third-party systems and phased from large taxpayers ([decree](http://mail.mfb.gov.mg/assets/file/communique/31674.pdf)). No collection figure under any of the three is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | A [National e-Payment Gateway](https://times.mw/the-year-ict-sector-grappled-with-implementation-woes/) is designed as the single entry point for all government collections, under a [fiscal governance programme](https://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/099050624155539128) of about US$80m. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The gateway was described as a 2025 breakthrough despite integration hurdles, supported by a financial inclusion project and a development bank; no live date, transaction figure or agency coverage is stated, and one dated account is all the base holds ([gateway](https://times.mw/the-year-ict-sector-grappled-with-implementation-woes/)).<br><br>A fiscal governance programme of SDR 60.3m, about US$80m equivalent at appraisal, supports revenue mobilisation, budget execution and procurement modernisation including e-procurement digitalisation ([programme](https://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/099050624155539128)); the base carries no implementation, disbursement or results reporting after the appraisal document ([programme](https://documents.worldbank.org/curated/en/099050624155539128)).</details> | Advanced |
| Mali | [TresorPay](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/mali-unveils-digital-platforms-for-payments-land-management) takes identity fees and fines, and about [FCFA 200m was collected digitally at civil registry centres](https://fassoactu.com/2026/07/31/transparence-financiere-les-collectivites-sommees-daccelerer-la-digitalisation-des-paiements/) between July 2024 and May 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The treasury payment service launched in March 2025 for identity fees and fines, with no transaction figure published ([service](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/mali-unveils-digital-platforms-for-payments-land-management)).<br><br>About FCFA 200m was collected at civil registry centres between July 2024 and May 2026, and every local authority has been ordered to report within thirty days ([collections](https://fassoactu.com/2026/07/31/transparence-financiere-les-collectivites-sommees-daccelerer-la-digitalisation-des-paiements/)). That reporting order is the base's only sign that the state is measuring what its own digital channels take.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritania | A [tax on electronic transactions](https://invest-time.com/2026/01/17/mauritanie-budget-2026-taxe-electro/) took effect in 2026, the [electronic tax platform](https://www.ami.mr/archives/265398) has 66 public bodies enrolled, and [electronic invoicing](https://www.ami.mr/archives/265398) was announced. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The 2026 finance law levies 0.1 per cent on electronic transactions above MRU 5,000 and 10 per cent on agent commissions, collected at source ([tax](https://invest-time.com/2026/01/17/mauritanie-budget-2026-taxe-electro/)).<br><br>The electronic tax services platform has 66 public bodies enrolled, 53 filing electronically and 3 paying through it, against 642 legally constituted public bodies of which 339 are active with the tax administration; more than 500 taxpayers overall are on the platform, producing 36.45 per cent of directorate revenue in 2026 ([platform](https://www.ami.mr/archives/265398)).<br><br>Electronic invoicing for public institutions was announced in July 2026 to handle value-added tax and withholding at source by transferring data automatically to the tax directorate, with no instrument, supplier or date published ([invoicing](https://www.ami.mr/archives/265398)).</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | Value-added tax on [digital services from foreign suppliers](https://www.mra.mu/eservices1/vat-eservices/vat-on-digital-or-electronic-services-from-foreign-suppliers) is being collected, with a supplier guide now at version 2.0. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The central bank's annual report for the year to June 2024 is the standing account of the payment system revenue runs over ([report](https://www.bom.mu/sites/default/files/bom_ar_2024_final.pdf)).<br><br>The revenue authority collects value-added tax on digital and electronic services supplied from abroad ([regime](https://www.mra.mu/eservices1/vat-eservices/vat-on-digital-or-electronic-services-from-foreign-suppliers)), and its guide for foreign suppliers is at version 2.0 ([guide](https://www.mra.mu/download/Guide_ForeignSupplier.pdf)).<br><br>No collection figure under the regime is published, and no count of registered foreign suppliers is held.</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | The fund attributes a [tax take of 24.6 per cent of output in 2025](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1marea2026001-source-pdf.pdf) to the full digitalisation of collection. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Customs went first: the administration [digitised revenue collection and banned cash for commercial clearance from 2019](https://www.douane.gov.ma/dms/loadDocument?documentId=78789).<br><br>The fund's [2026 consultation](https://www.imf.org/-/media/files/publications/cr/2026/english/1marea2026001-source-pdf.pdf) puts the result at a tax take of 24.6 per cent of national output in 2025, attributed to full digitalisation of collection — the clearest fiscal return on a digital system the base holds for any unit. The tax directorate's [own procedure page](https://portail.tax.gov.ma/wps/portal/DGI/Vos-impots-procedures/Autres-demandes) shows two online routes to a tax identifier.</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | An [online tax clearance certificate](https://mznews.co.mz/en/autoridade-tributaria-lanca-solicitacao-online-da-certidao-de-quitacao-fiscal-a-partir-de-3-de-agosto/) went live in August 2026 with a ten working-day ceiling, and a [tax amendment on wallet-agent commissions](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-revises-irps-code-to-tax-commissions-of-m-pesa-e-mola-and-m-kesh-agents/) is in preparation. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The certificate is requested through the taxpayer portal and issued within a maximum of ten working days, with a published electronic complaints address; the authority's own communique is not held ([certificate](https://mznews.co.mz/en/autoridade-tributaria-lanca-solicitacao-online-da-certidao-de-quitacao-fiscal-a-partir-de-3-de-agosto/)).<br><br>An amendment on the taxation of mobile-money agent commissions is still in preparation, with taxation already proceeding under the existing framework and 787 wallet agents registered for tax numbers in the first half of 2025; no adoption, tabling or gazetting is held ([amendment](https://clubofmozambique.com/news/mozambique-revises-irps-code-to-tax-commissions-of-m-pesa-e-mola-and-m-kesh-agents/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | The revenue agency collected [N$98.9 billion gross](https://www.namibian.com.na/one-million-taxpayers-carry-the-nation/) in 2024/25 from 1.0 million registered taxpayers, 575,136 of them electronic filers. <details><summary>Full record</summary>At 31 March 2025 the agency had 1.0 million registered taxpayers, of whom 575,136 were registered as electronic filers, on 1.04 million active tax accounts ([annual report](https://www.namibian.com.na/one-million-taxpayers-carry-the-nation/)). Gross revenue that year was N$98.9 billion and net N$88.555 billion ([annual report](https://www.namibian.com.na/one-million-taxpayers-carry-the-nation/)). The year before it recorded 477,164 electronic filers ([2023/24](https://www.namra.org.na/documents/cms/uploaded/namra-annual-report--20234-b0ca93ea50.pdf)).<br><br>A trader identification number became mandatory on every import and export declaration on the customs platform from 1 April 2026, tying the customs register to the taxpayer register ([notice](https://www.namra.org.na/documents/cms/uploaded/public-notice--mandatory-tin-on-asycudaworld--9-dec-2025-7d1548a64e.pdf)).<br><br>A tax amnesty had brought in 28,157 taxpayers and N$2.6 billion by 30 September 2025 ([amnesty](https://www.namra.org.na/documents/cms/uploaded/namra--newsletter-july--september-2025-f477ca628d.pdf)).<br><br>The integrated tax administration system is in service, and electronic filing covers rather more than half of those registered.</details> | Advanced |
