| Niger | [Identity-fee payment kiosks](https://www.actuniger.com/societe/21937-carte-nationale-didentite-biometrique-cnib-avec-nita-les-demarches-simplifiees-pour-regler-ses-frais-et-se-faire-enroler-en-toute-serenite.html) were deployed at enrolment sites in April 2026, issuing receipts for the enrolment file. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The kiosks went live at enrolment sites on 27 April 2026 ([kiosks](https://www.actuniger.com/societe/21937-carte-nationale-didentite-biometrique-cnib-avec-nita-les-demarches-simplifiees-pour-regler-ses-frais-et-se-faire-enroler-en-toute-serenite.html)).<br><br>They are the state's only digital collection channel on this record, and they collect for the identity programme rather than for general revenue. No transaction count or value is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Nigeria | [NGN 27.1 trillion was collected](https://techcabal.com/2026/08/11/nigeria-collected-94-million-in-taxes-daily/) between January and July 2026, credited to digitised systems, with a [treasury system](https://techafricanews.com/2025/03/06/nigeria-launches-treasury-management-system-to-enhance-revenue-and-budget-efficiency) deducting automatically and [7.5 per cent VAT on electronic banking fees](https://www.vanguardngr.com/2026/01/fg-directs-banks-fintechs-to-collect-7-5-vat-on-electronic-banking-fees/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>Collection ran at NGN 27.1 trillion between January and July 2026, an average NGN 127.83bn a day and 95.76 per cent of the whole of 2025's collection, which the revenue service credits to digitised tax systems, four reform laws and an executive order closing loopholes ([collection](https://techcabal.com/2026/08/11/nigeria-collected-94-million-in-taxes-daily/)); the attribution is the service's own and no counterfactual or system-level breakdown is published ([collection](https://techcabal.com/2026/08/11/nigeria-collected-94-million-in-taxes-daily/)).<br><br>The treasury management and revenue assurance system automatically deducts and remits value-added tax, withholding tax and stamp duty on vendor payments across all agencies ([treasury](https://techafricanews.com/2025/03/06/nigeria-launches-treasury-management-system-to-enhance-revenue-and-budget-efficiency)).<br><br>Value-added tax of 7.5 per cent on electronic banking fees took effect in January 2026 ([tax](https://www.vanguardngr.com/2026/01/fg-directs-banks-fintechs-to-collect-7-5-vat-on-electronic-banking-fees/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | A [ministerial order of April 2026](https://www.rra.gov.rw/fileadmin/Folder_for_2025/Ministerial_Order_VAT_on_Online_Services_April_2026.pdf) requires financial institutions facilitating online payments to integrate with tax administration systems; collection ran at [101.9 per cent of target](https://www.rra.gov.rw/fileadmin/Folder_for_2025/ANNUAL_REPORT_2024-2025_Final_version.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The revenue authority's own annual report records 101.9 per cent of the revenue target collected in 2024/25, with electronic tax filing, billing machines, mobile declaration and online payment and refund services in continuing operation ([report](https://www.rra.gov.rw/fileadmin/Folder_for_2025/ANNUAL_REPORT_2024-2025_Final_version.pdf)).<br><br>The order made on 29 April 2026 requires financial institutions facilitating online payments to integrate with the tax administration's systems ([order](https://www.rra.gov.rw/fileadmin/Folder_for_2025/Ministerial_Order_VAT_on_Online_Services_April_2026.pdf)).<br><br>On the consumer side, over RWF 464 million was paid to 40,905 people who requested electronic invoices, using the reward loop as a compliance mechanism ([disbursement](https://www.ktpress.rw/2025/08/revenue-authority-disburses-additional-rwf464-million-in-vat-rewards/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Senegal | The tax authority [withdrew the paper fiscal stamp](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/tech/le-senegal-supprime-le-timbre-fiscal-physique-au-profit-dune-version-numerique) on 20 July 2026, and a [provider suspended its free electronic stamp service](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/diotali-suspend-son-service-gratuit-de-timbre-fiscal-electronique-et-annonce-le-remboursement-de-ses-usagers/) on 28 July citing an administrative decision outside its control. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The withdrawal made the digital service the sole recognised channel. It is removal of the analogue route rather than addition of a digital one: unused paper stamps were exchangeable until 15 August 2026, and no offline or assisted route is stated ([stamp](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/actualites/tech/le-senegal-supprime-le-timbre-fiscal-physique-au-profit-dune-version-numerique), [channel](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/diotali-suspend-son-service-gratuit-de-timbre-fiscal-electronique-et-annonce-le-remboursement-de-ses-usagers/)).<br><br>Eight days later a provider suspended its free tier, citing an administrative decision outside its control. It is the first consumer-cost consequence of state-platform exclusivity the base holds, and it is not stated whether the decision was addressed to that provider or to the distribution channel generally ([suspension](https://lesoleil.sn/actualites/diotali-suspend-son-service-gratuit-de-timbre-fiscal-electronique-et-annonce-le-remboursement-de-ses-usagers/)).<br><br>A levy on mobile-money transfers was proposed under a recovery plan to raise about FCFA 220bn over three years; both sources post-date the window's start, so the proposal's earlier position cannot be stated ([levy](https://www.ecofinagency.com/news/0812-51194-gsma-maps-the-reforms-required-for-senegal-s-digital-takeoff)).</details> | Mixed, the paper fiscal stamp withdrawn in favour of a digital channel while the free tier of that channel was suspended eight days later |
| Sierra Leone | The revenue authority [began distributing smart tax machines to 10,000 registered businesses](https://thecalabashnewspaper.com/archives/63975) from 31 August 2026, alongside the existing electronic cash register system. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Nationwide distribution of smart goods and services tax machines to 10,000 registered businesses began on 31 August 2026 ([distribution](https://thecalabashnewspaper.com/archives/63975)). The commissioner-general said the machines complement the existing electronic cash register system and are intended to support accurate record-keeping, strengthen tax compliance, let businesses issue valid receipts and reduce revenue leakages, as part of a domestic revenue mobilisation drive ([distribution](https://thecalabashnewspaper.com/archives/63975)).<br><br>The machines are counted against no published total of registered businesses, no distribution schedule, no cost and no baseline for the leakage they are to reduce.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | A [binding circular](https://mof.gov.so/sites/default/files/Publications/Circular%20reff%20434_0001.pdf) made digital revenue receipts mandatory from January 2026, and the [audited annual report](https://mof.gov.so/sites/default/files/Publications/FGS_Annual_Report%20_%26_Consolidated_Financial_Statements.pdf) records digitalisation among 2025's reforms. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The finance ministry's publications register records a binding formal circular mandating the use of digital revenue receipts, released on 4 January 2026 ([circular](https://mof.gov.so/sites/default/files/Publications/Circular%20reff%20434_0001.pdf)). The document is an image-only scan whose text was recovered by character recognition.<br><br>The audited annual report and consolidated financial statements for 2025 describe digitalisation of public finance regulations and revenue reforms as an achievement of the year, naming a non-tax revenue portal, an electronic tax administration system and customs-to-financial-management integration ([report](https://mof.gov.so/sites/default/files/Publications/FGS_Annual_Report%20_%26_Consolidated_Financial_Statements.pdf)).<br><br>No collection figure attributable to the digital channel is published.</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | The revenue service [published a VAT modernisation consultation paper](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/sars-invites-input-on-vat-modernisation/DZQ58vV8m9NMzXy2) proposing electronic invoicing, an interoperability framework and electronic reporting, [with feedback due 16 October 2026](https://www.sars.gov.za/vat-modernisation-consultation-paper-august-2026/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08 — the revenue service [released a VAT modernisation consultation paper](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/sars-invites-input-on-vat-modernisation/DZQ58vV8m9NMzXy2) proposing a digital VAT model combining electronic invoicing, an interoperability framework and electronic reporting, aimed at moving administration off manual processes and retrospective verification and into the systems businesses already run. It follows a 2023 discussion paper and is stated as building blocks rather than a decided design.<br><br>The fuller account [describes a five-corner model moving toward VAT auto-assessment, with a phased rollout from 2026/27 and stakeholder feedback due 16 October 2026](https://www.sars.gov.za/vat-modernisation-consultation-paper-august-2026/).<br><br>It is a consultation paper, not a rule: no cost to business, exemption threshold, accreditation regime for service providers or legislative vehicle is stated. What has advanced is a published design intention on the largest transaction flow the state touches.<br><br>2026-08-31 — the home affairs department [re-advertised its revenue administration system over 275 fixed sites and 230 mobile units, replacing a generic 400-plus listing and making point-of-sale devices and mobile offices the primary transaction channels](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/top-ict-tenders-home-affairs-expands-revenue-admin-tender/dgp45MaBlAbqX9l8), with [only suppliers on the state IT agency's RFB 1183 transversal contract eligible to bid](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/top-ict-tenders-home-affairs-expands-revenue-admin-tender/dgp45MaBlAbqX9l8). It is a tender, not an award: no value, closing date or bidder is held.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | [Cash payment of tax at revenue offices was barred](https://www.eyeradio.org/revenue-authority-changes-tax-payment-process/) with immediate effect in July 2026, after [cash was abolished for passport and identity payments](https://www.sudanspost.com/south-sudan-introduces-digital-payment-system-for-passports-after-supply-crisis/) in January. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The economic reform committee ordered banks to enforce cash tax payments ([directive](https://jubaechotv.com.ss/economic-reform-committee-orders-banks-to-enforce-cash-tax-payments/)), reversing the direction the cashless measure set.<br><br>The tax measure was made by resolution of a ministerial committee on economic reform, framed by the authority as a transparency and liquidity response and stated as temporary until further notice ([resolution](https://www.eyeradio.org/revenue-authority-changes-tax-payment-process/)).<br><br>Cash was abolished for passport and identity payments after a supply crisis in January 2026 ([passports](https://www.sudanspost.com/south-sudan-introduces-digital-payment-system-for-passports-after-supply-crisis/)).<br><br>Both are removals of the cash channel rather than additions of a digital one, in an economy widely described as overwhelmingly cash-based, and neither states an assisted or offline route for anyone without an account.</details> | Regressed |
| Sudan | An [electronic government-collection system](https://sudanhorizon.com/finance-ministry-package-of-reform-policies-without-imposing-new-burdens/) is named among the reforms behind a stated rise in first-half 2026 revenue, and the ministry states no figure, baseline or attribution method behind the claim. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system is contracted through a national bank ([contract](https://www.suna.sd/posts/shrak-byn-almaly-obnk-alaamal-alotny-ltkdym-khdm-aysaly)) and has been transferred between states ([transfer](https://www.suna.sd/posts/taazyza-lltaaaon-almaly-mdyr-aaam-maly-alkdarf-ystkbl-ofd-olay-ksla-lbhth-alyat-althsyl-alalktrony-201)), with one state's collection rate praised by the minister ([praise](https://www.suna.sd/posts/ozyr-almaly-yshyd-bghod-olay-alnyl-alabyd-fy-ttbyk-althsyl-alaalktrony-odaam-altnmy-alzraaay)).<br><br>That state's figure of more than 90 per cent is the state's own and unverified, and the ministry states no revenue figure, baseline or attribution method behind the claimed rise ([reforms](https://sudanhorizon.com/finance-ministry-package-of-reform-policies-without-imposing-new-burdens/), [directives](https://www.suna.sd/posts/finance-minister-issues-directives-to-modernize-ministry-performance)).<br><br>A collection system spreading state by state is the most concrete delivery in this unit; the revenue claim attached to it is not testable from anything published.</details> | Advanced |
| Tanzania | A [mandatory electronic payments order](https://fbattorneys.co.tz/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/1784910470319-1.pdf) made nine scheduled transaction classes electronic-payment-only, an order under the electronic transactions Act rather than regulations, and cash remains legal tender. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The order came into operation in 2026 and is widely mis-reported as regulations ([order](https://fbattorneys.co.tz/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/1784910470319-1.pdf), [drive](https://www.thecitizen.co.tz/tanzania/business/tanzania-steps-up-cash-lite-drive-as-digital-payments-hit-new-highs-5541316)).<br><br>Mandatory electronic settlement for named transactions was explained to traders under the 2026/27 tax law changes; traders at the same meeting asked for cheaper electronic transaction charges, and no commencement date or penalty is stated in the record held ([settlement](https://ippmedia.co.tz/nipashe/biashara/read/wafanyabiashara-waomba-gharama-nafuu-za-miamala-ya-kielektroniki-2026-08-19-194833)).<br><br>A central electronic ticketing system carries transport ministry figures to March 2026 alongside a companion vehicle tracking system on 13,843 buses ([ticketing](https://dailynews.co.tz/cashless-shift-poised-to-transform-economy/)); the figures are the ministry's, reported at second hand, and no earlier count is held for either ([ticketing](https://dailynews.co.tz/cashless-shift-poised-to-transform-economy/)).<br><br>Zanzibar moved a fare box and a set of municipal charges onto one wallet: fares on the fifteen electric buses that entered service on 1 August 2026 are paid by smart card, and the same platform carries municipal parking fees, traffic fines and state payments to clove farmers ([Zanzibar](https://www.thecitizen.co.tz/tanzania/zanzibar/zanzibar-shifts-quietly-from-cash-to-fast-digital-payment-systems-5572738)).</details> | Advanced |
| Uganda | Expressway tolls are [still collected in cash](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/mps-want-ura-to-take-over-toll-collections-of-NV_239482_082026), with a parliamentary committee recommending a revenue-authority takeover and an electronic payment system. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The Kampala-Entebbe Expressway concession remains reliant on cash. A parliamentary infrastructure committee recommended on 25 August 2026 that the revenue authority take over collection and introduce an electronic payment system, called for a forensic audit of the concession's operations and revenue collections, and asked for scrutiny of a reported UGX 36 billion contract extension; the ICT minister of state told the House that the operator has never given government the source code or password of the system ([committee](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/news/mps-want-ura-to-take-over-toll-collections-of-NV_239482_082026)).</details> | No change |
| Zambia | The [government payment gateway](https://itweb.africa/article/digital-services-strengthen-zambia-finances/JBwEr7n3zk3M6Db2) puts cumulative collections at over K8.8 billion with no stated start date, and the two [digital revenue figures](https://zambianbusinesstimes.com/k22-million-collected-from-8-councils-via-electronic-payments/) cover different periods and issuers. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The collection total has no stated start date, so it is a cumulative figure of unknown period ([gateway](https://itweb.africa/article/digital-services-strengthen-zambia-finances/JBwEr7n3zk3M6Db2), [appraisal](https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099012025023033676/txt/P505094-630a5ef9-d23a-4221-b7c8-397c94a90b4d.txt), [property](https://techafricanews.com/2026/05/13/zambia-launches-digital-property-management-system-to-improve-public-service-delivery/)).<br><br>The two revenue figures cover different periods and issuers and are not a like-for-like series ([revenue](https://zambianbusinesstimes.com/k22-million-collected-from-8-councils-via-electronic-payments/), [investment](https://www.zanis.gov.zm/?p=3243), [finances](https://itweb.africa/article/digital-services-strengthen-zambia-finances/JBwEr7n3zk3M6Db2)).<br><br>A cumulative total of unknown period and two non-comparable figures are what the revenue record supports.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | [Radio-frequency toll tag express lanes](https://263chat.com/zimbabwe-widens-key-tollgates-as-e-toll-era-begins/) opened at two of 29 plazas, with nothing stated on retention, access or legal basis for the movement record the tags generate. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The first plaza was handed over in July 2026 ([tolls](https://263chat.com/zimbabwe-widens-key-tollgates-as-e-toll-era-begins/)).<br><br>Nothing is stated on retention, access or legal basis for the movement record the tags generate — a vehicle-location dataset created by a revenue measure, in a unit with a licensing regime for data controllers that this system's operator would fall under.<br><br>A taxpayer registration self-service portal is readying a second phase of the wider revenue management system, integrating third-party stakeholders; that detail is paywalled beyond the free lede ([portal](https://fingaz.co.zw/2026/08/06/zimra-readies-tarms-phase-2-rollout/)).</details> | Advanced |

## B2B and B2G functionality

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The tax directorate [launched remote electronic payment](https://www.aps.dz/fr/economie/banque-et-finances/ms0wxt1l-lancement-du-paiement-electronique-a-distance-des-droits-et-taxes) in July 2026, completing the declare-and-pay path online. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The tax portal was established under [the 2022 finance law](https://www.joradp.dz/FTP/jo-francais/2021/F2021100.pdf), and the directorate's own [guidance on opening a tax file is the public-facing description of how a taxpayer enters the register it runs on](https://www.mfdgi.gov.dz/fr/professionnels/nouveau-contribuable-fr/ouvrir-dossier-fiscal).<br><br>2026-01-21 - [remote declaration became obligatory](https://www.aps.dz/fr/economie/commerce-et-service/mkodg3kj-direction-generale-des-impots-obligation-de-souscription-en-ligne-des-declarations-fiscales-a-partir-du-1er-janvier-en-cours).<br><br>2026-07-25 - [remote electronic payment was launched](https://www.aps.dz/fr/economie/banque-et-finances/ms0wxt1l-lancement-du-paiement-electronique-a-distance-des-droits-et-taxes), and a joint finance and commerce protocol had [launched electronic payment of stamp duties for commercial-register formalities in December 2025](https://www.aps.dz/fr/economie/commerce-et-service/mjkd73l9-lancement-du-service-de-paiement-des-droits-de-timbre-par-cartes-cib-et-edahabia). No transaction volume or take-up figure is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | A [second phase of the retail payment switch and a government cash-deposit system are named in the 2026/27 estimates](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/Ministry%20of%20FInance%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%2026-02-2026%20-%20Final.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2003 — [the National Clearance and Settlement Systems Act empowers the central bank to recognise and regulate clearance and settlement systems](https://www.bankofbotswana.bw/sites/default/files/National%20Clearance%20and%20Settlement%20Systems%20%20Act%202003%20%28Website%20Download%29_1.pdf), the statutory basis for the interbank rails.<br><br>2026-02-26 — [the finance ministry named a second phase of the national retail payment switch and a new central-bank digital cash-deposit system for government revenue collection](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/Ministry%20of%20FInance%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%2026-02-2026%20-%20Final.pdf).<br><br>Neither carries an operator, cost or go-live date on the record, and no business-to-government transaction volume is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Burundi | A national instant payment system [launched in April 2026 with interoperability across banks, microfinance and payment institutions](https://www.brb.bi/node/3293). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-09-18 — [managers of public funds were required to obtain an electronic-billing-machine invoice from suppliers before payment, on pain of a 20 per cent penalty](https://www.obr.bi/index.php/actualites/2357-la-facture-emise-par-la-machine-a-facturation-electronique-une-exigence-de-tous-les-gestionnaires-des-fonds-publics-aupres-de-leurs-fournisseurs-de-biens-et-services) — a digital control placed inside the government-to-supplier payment cycle.<br><br>2026-04-23 — [the national instant payment system was launched, built under the World Bank-supported foundations project and providing full interoperability between commercial banks, microfinance institutions and payment institutions](https://www.brb.bi/node/3293).<br><br>[The central bank reports annually on the clearing house, the real-time gross settlement system, the national switch and electronic money](https://www.brb.bi/sites/default/files/2025-11/Rapport%20annuel%20sur%20les%20syst%C3%A8mes%20et%20moyens%20de%20%20paiement%20et%20leur%20surveillance.pdf), so a series exists; no transaction volume or value for the new instant system is yet published.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | Interoperable merchant payments ran at [48,605 transactions worth FCFA 9.7bn in the first quarter of 2026](https://leconomie.info/paiement-marchand-moins-de-1-des-transactions-interoperables-enregistrees-en-zone-cemac-au-premier-trimestre-2026/), under 1% of switch volume. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The first-quarter 2026 acquiring and issuing tables record [48,605 transactions worth FCFA 9.7bn, under 1% of the switch's 2025 volume, at an average ticket of FCFA 199,485 against FCFA 42,512 across all flows](https://leconomie.info/paiement-marchand-moins-de-1-des-transactions-interoperables-enregistrees-en-zone-cemac-au-premier-trimestre-2026/).<br><br>The concentration is extreme: [two mobile operators together account for 98% of issuing value and one bank for 71% of acquiring value](https://leconomie.info/paiement-marchand-moins-de-1-des-transactions-interoperables-enregistrees-en-zone-cemac-au-premier-trimestre-2026/). A merchant rail with an average ticket nearly five times the all-flows average is being used for large payments between businesses rather than for retail acceptance, which is what the tariff consultation concedes.</details> | Advanced |
| Central African Republic | An operator and a customs supplier [launched automated mobile payment of the import declaration fee](https://investirencentrafrique.com/2026/07/03/orange-centrafrique-et-webb-fontaine-lancent-le-paiement-automatise-de-la-redevance-dpi-via-orange-money/) in July 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-03 - an operator, a customs technology supplier and a state entity [launched automated mobile-money payment of the prior import declaration fee, with automatic generation of the declaration reference](https://investirencentrafrique.com/2026/07/03/orange-centrafrique-et-webb-fontaine-lancent-le-paiement-automatise-de-la-redevance-dpi-via-orange-money/). It is a business-to-government rail on the customs chain and the clearest one the base holds.<br><br>No transaction volume, value or share of declarations paid this way is published. The other government-facing rail on the record runs the other way: [mobile-money salary payment for officials outside the capital](https://corbeaunews-centrafrique.org/centrafrique-a-batangafo-un-mois-sans-reseau-orange-bloque-les-communications-et-les-services-financiers/), which the same ledger carries as not held.</details> | Advanced |
| Chad | An [interoperable payments platform partnership](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/breves/breves-simple/la-fintech-cashi-s-allie-a-la-sfi-pour-accelerer-les-paiements-numeriques-au-tchad) was announced with an international finance institution. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The partnership was announced in April 2026 ([partnership](https://www.wearetech.africa/fr/fils/breves/breves-simple/la-fintech-cashi-s-allie-a-la-sfi-pour-accelerer-les-paiements-numeriques-au-tchad)).<br><br>No value, scope, timetable or participant list is published. It is the only business-facing payments instrument in this ledger, against three licensed wallets whose usage is unmeasured.</details> | Advanced |
| Comoros | A [decentralised digital financial institution was licensed in February 2026](https://itweb.africa/article/axian-gets-digital-first-financial-license-in-the-comoros/xA9POvNElGOqo4J8), and an [agent-banking network is still in test](https://banque-comores.km/uploads/BCC-livret%283%29.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-02 - the central bank [licensed a separately regulated entity under prudential supervision offering mobile nano- and micro-credit alongside the group's existing wallet, on a branchless model](https://itweb.africa/article/axian-gets-digital-first-financial-license-in-the-comoros/xA9POvNElGOqo4J8). It is [company-announced and reproduced near-verbatim by both outlets](https://techafricanews.com/2026/02/24/axian-gains-central-bank-approval-to-launch-regulated-digital-finance-institution-in-comoros/); no central-bank statement, licence instrument or regulation establishing the category is held, and the underlying payment-services and electronic-money statute had been adopted five years earlier.<br><br>The agent-banking network has been [in test since June 2025 with agents identified and trained and progressive commissioning under way, two microfinance institutions not having signed the conventions](https://banque-comores.km/uploads/BCC-livret%283%29.pdf) - eleven months in test with no live date published.</details> | Advanced |
| Cote d'Ivoire | The [treasury's mobile payment platform](https://website.tresormoney.ci/navs-link/s/26/CONTEXTE-DE-CRÉATION) was built to digitalise all state payment and collection, and [reached public hospitals in 2026](https://tresor.gouv.ci/tres/tresorpay-tresormoney-le-tresor-public-accelere-la-modernisation-des-paiements-dans-les-etablissements-publics-de-sante/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The treasury's own account of the platform's origin runs from [a revenue-collection service for local authorities and a 2019 decree instituting electronic collection of revenue in communes, regions and districts, to the build of a mobile payment platform with a state financial subsidiary to digitalise all state payment, collection and receipt mechanisms](https://website.tresormoney.ci/navs-link/s/26/CONTEXTE-DE-CRÉATION).<br><br>2026-05-28 - it was [extended into public health establishments for electronic collection of hospital fees on a named short code, with public accountants, hospital agents and cashiers trained](https://tresor.gouv.ci/tres/tresorpay-tresormoney-le-tresor-public-accelere-la-modernisation-des-paiements-dans-les-etablissements-publics-de-sante/). The claimed effects are reduced cash handling, automated remittance and traceability of receipts. That leg is citizen-to-government rather than business-to-government.<br><br>No transaction volume, value or participating-institution count is published for the platform on any leg.</details> | Advanced |
| Djibouti | The [settlement platform has combined real-time gross settlement and automated clearing since 2021](https://banque-centrale.dj/infrastructures-ats/), the Treasury a participant. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The wholesale layer is in place and unchanged across the period: the central bank's [platform combines a real-time gross settlement system and an automated clearing house, covering interbank settlement with the Treasury among the participants](https://banque-centrale.dj/infrastructures-ats/) - the route by which a business pays a bank or the state.<br><br>The one measure inside it is a tightening of instruments rather than of function: from [31 August 2025 cheques had to meet new format, security and encoding standards or be refused by every clearing channel](https://banque-centrale.dj/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/BCD-Communique-SYRAD.pdf).<br><br>No transaction volume, value or participant list is published, and no electronic-invoicing or government payment portal appears in the record.</details> | No change |
| DR Congo | A [partnership agreement covers connecting microfinance institutions and cooperatives to the national switch across 145 territories](https://actualite.cd/2026/04/16/rdc-le-projet-transforme-appuie-la-digitalisation-des-imf-et-coopec). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-04 - a payments firm and two sector associations [signed on core banking, enterprise resource planning and card modules, 10,000 payment terminals with biometric authentication, chip cards, and connection of microfinance institutions and cooperatives to the national switch across 145 territories](https://actualite.cd/2026/04/16/rdc-le-projet-transforme-appuie-la-digitalisation-des-imf-et-coopec).<br><br>Nothing is deployed on file. The switch connection is the load-bearing item and no go-live is held; the arrangement is externally financed, and no source names the operator of the switch, the connection standard or what an institution must meet to join.</details> | Advanced |
| Egypt | A [digital factoring portal lets companies verify prior funding of an invoice electronically](https://www.youm7.com/story/2026/8/6/%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D8%A7%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D9%84%D9%82-%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AD%D9%84%D8%A9-%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF%D8%A9-%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%D9%86%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AE%D8%B5%D9%8A%D9%85-%D9%88%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B5%D8%A9-%D8%A5%D9%84%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%B1%D9%88%D9%86%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B9/7504876), enforcing a ban on double-funding a receivable. <details><summary>Full record</summary>There was no electronic check on whether an invoice had already been funded. Factoring companies now [verify prior funding electronically through a direct link with the finance ministry and the tax authority, the platform vetting invoices before financing and freezing them to the financing company to enforce a board resolution's ban on double-funding a receivable, with a second phase to make the full cycle electronic undated](https://www.youm7.com/story/2026/8/6/%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D8%A7%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D9%84%D9%82-%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AD%D9%84%D8%A9-%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF%D8%A9-%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%D9%86%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AE%D8%B5%D9%8A%D9%85-%D9%88%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B5%D8%A9-%D8%A5%D9%84%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%B1%D9%88%D9%86%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B9/7504876); companies were [trained on it on 6 August 2026](https://www.dailynewsegypt.com/2026/02/08/egypts-fra-launches-digital-factoring-portal-to-curb-financing-risks). [Factored paper value rose 77.8% in 2025 to EGP 132.2bn](https://www.youm7.com/story/2026/8/6/%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D8%A7%D8%A8%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%85%D8%A7%D9%84%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D8%B7%D9%84%D9%82-%D9%85%D8%B1%D8%AD%D9%84%D8%A9-%D8%AC%D8%AF%D9%8A%D8%AF%D8%A9-%D9%84%D8%B1%D9%82%D9%85%D9%86%D8%A9-%D8%A7%D9%84%D8%AA%D8%AE%D8%B5%D9%8A%D9%85-%D9%88%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B5%D8%A9-%D8%A5%D9%84%D9%83%D8%AA%D8%B1%D9%88%D9%86%D9%8A%D8%A9-%D8%AA%D9%85%D9%86%D8%B9/7504876).<br><br>2026-08-13 - the [state digital financial infrastructure operator agreed the full acquisition of a non-bank lender to small and medium firms, extending its own infrastructure into direct lending](https://www.zawya.com/en/press-release/companies-news/efg-hermes-advises-efinance-on-strategic-acquisition-of-tamweely-financial-services-430407), with [no consideration disclosed](https://www.zawya.com/en/press-release/companies-news/efg-hermes-advises-efinance-on-strategic-acquisition-of-tamweely-financial-services-430407); the record states no separation between its role building and running government payment infrastructure and the lending book it is buying.</details> | Advanced |
| Equatorial Guinea | [Cash collection of taxes and fees is prohibited](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/orden_de_la_presidencia_del_gobierno_relativa_a_la_recaudacion_o_cobro_en_efectivo_de_impuestos_tasas_y_demas_ingresos_de_la_administracion), and [chamber registration is required before any state contract or payment](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/orden_para_impulsar_la_inscripcion_de_los_operadores_economicos_en_las_camaras_oficiales_de_comercio). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [presidential order of September 2025](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/orden_de_la_presidencia_del_gobierno_relativa_a_la_recaudacion_o_cobro_en_efectivo_de_impuestos_tasas_y_demas_ingresos_de_la_administracion) routes all public revenue to bank accounts or electronic platforms, which makes business-to-government payment electronic by default.<br><br>On the identification side, [registration with the chambers of commerce became a precondition of any state contract or payment from 1 January 2026](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/orden_para_impulsar_la_inscripcion_de_los_operadores_economicos_en_las_camaras_oficiales_de_comercio). The procurement system that would carry the transaction is [still a bill](https://www.guineaecuatorialpress.com/noticias/inicio_del_estudio_en_lectura_unica_del_proyecto_de_ley_de_contratos_del_sector_publico).</details> | Advanced |
| Eswatini | All bank and non-bank participants are connected to the [payment switch's](https://parliament.gov.sz/media/speeches/budget/2026_27%20FY%20Budget_Speech_2026%20FINAL.pdf) round-the-clock fast-payments platform, on a wholesale system running since 2007. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The interbank payment and settlement system has operated under its 2007 rulebook since then, and is the wholesale rail beneath business and government payments ([rulebook](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/wp-content/uploads/2021/03/SWIPSS-Rules-Procedures.pdf)).<br><br>The finance minister told Parliament in February 2026 that all bank and non-bank participants are connected to the payment switch's 24-hour fast-payments platform ([budget speech](https://parliament.gov.sz/media/speeches/budget/2026_27%20FY%20Budget_Speech_2026%20FINAL.pdf)); the eighth and final participant went live on 15 November 2025 ([remarks](https://www.centralbank.org.sz/wp-content/uploads/2021/04/Governors-Welcoming-Remarks-SIIPS-in-Africa-2025-Report-Launch-November-2025.pdf)).<br><br>The FinTech Strategy 2025-2030 targets 80 per cent interoperability by 2028 ([strategy](https://www.times.co.sz/business/readmore.php?bhsadjgfoh=FinTech+Strategy+to+transform+Eswatini%E2%80%99s+digital+future&bvhdgsj=Business+and+Economy&yiphi=2100)).<br><br>Against the rails, more than 90 per cent of person-to-business payments are still cash ([survey](https://www.biometricupdate.com/202508/robust-dpi-inevitable-for-eswatinis-economic-jobs-boom-world-bank)), and no business-to-business or business-to-government transaction volume is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Ethiopia | Federal public bodies must now accept payment from any licensed provider ([directive](https://www.mofed.gov.et/media/filer_public/7e/a5/7ea5bf66-de9b-447d-b34d-8ca9667ddf50/1069_2017_yamanegesete_maseriyaa_beetoce_faqaade_katasathaacawe_yakefeyaa_agalegelote_sachiwoce_kefeyaa_yamiqabalubatene.pdf)), and an electronic invoicing layer was established. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Directive 1069/2025, signed by the finance minister in May 2025, requires all federal public bodies to accept payment from any licensed payment service provider — cards, prepaid instruments, mobile wallets, mobile and internet banking and bank transfers — and to integrate their collection systems with authorised gateways ([directive](https://www.mofed.gov.et/media/filer_public/7e/a5/7ea5bf66-de9b-447d-b34d-8ca9667ddf50/1069_2017_yamanegesete_maseriyaa_beetoce_faqaade_katasathaacawe_yakefeyaa_agalegelote_sachiwoce_kefeyaa_yamiqabalubatene.pdf)).<br><br>Directive 1142/2026 established the electronic invoicing administration layer, requiring taxpayers to use an authorised electronic invoice registration system and setting rules for sales-registration system providers ([directive](https://justice.gov.et/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/1142_የኤሌክትሮኒክ_ደረሰኝ_ሥርዓት_አስተዳደር_መመሪያ_ቁጥር_1142_2018.pdf)).<br><br>The national payments conference of December 2025 recorded the launch of the draft national digital payments strategy and of the instant payment system, the rails both directives depend on ([conference](https://edpc.nbe.gov.et/)).<br><br>The base holds the obligations, not compliance: nothing counts the public bodies that have integrated.</details> | Advanced |
| Gambia | The [instant payment switch](https://www.gamswitch.com/press-release/the-gambia-launches-bantaba-2-0-its-first-real-time-interoperable-payment-system) links banks, mobile money and fintechs, and [a revenue platform](https://op.gov.gm/good-governance-impacting-economic-growth) is unifying government collections. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [switch launched in December 2025](https://www.gamswitch.com/press-release/the-gambia-launches-bantaba-2-0-its-first-real-time-interoperable-payment-system) is the country's first real-time interoperable rail across banks, mobile money and fintechs, on a phased rollout.<br><br>On the government side a [revenue platform to unify payments across ministries and agencies is advancing](https://op.gov.gm/good-governance-impacting-economic-growth), per the State House readout of March 2026, and the revenue authority's [2023 self-assessment](https://mofea.gov.gm/wp-content/uploads/2024/02/2023-PFM-Annual-Progress-Report.pdf) had already put a tax payment interface live. Six banks are [live on the regional cross-border system](https://papss.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/PAPSS-LIST-OF-LIVE-COMMERCIAL-BANKS_APRIL2026.pdf).</details> | Advanced |
| Ghana | The central bank puts the small-business financing gap at [US$4.8bn a year](https://www.graphic.com.gh/news/general-news/ghana-news-bog-to-leverage-digital-transaction-data-to-support-smes.html) and proposes digital transaction data as the underwriting route to close it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The figure is the bank's own, published in speeches rather than in a series, with no methodology, sample or date range given ([financing gap](https://www.graphic.com.gh/news/general-news/ghana-news-bog-to-leverage-digital-transaction-data-to-support-smes.html), [digital lending](https://www.myjoyonline.com/bog-identifies-4-8bn-annual-sme-financing-gap-pushes-digital-data-driven-lending/)).<br><br>The proposal attached to it, lending underwritten on mobile-money and other digital transaction records rather than collateral, is stated as a direction and carries no instrument, timetable or participating lender.</details> | No change, a first measurement with no earlier figure behind it |
| Guinea-Bissau | Importers must [declare and pay value added tax through the platform](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/api/public_files/LX4zIpsBZ7DXb_HFUy3A.pdf) to clear customs, from December 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [circular of December 2025](https://kontaktu.mef.gw/api/public_files/LX4zIpsBZ7DXb_HFUy3A.pdf) makes business-to-government payment electronic by compulsion: importers declare the tax they have borne on the customs declaration and pay exclusively through the platform.<br><br>The cross-border trade layer is being built regionally: a [Bissau workshop presented the union's paperless customs directive and a planned trade-document exchange platform](https://ang.gw/uemoa-bissau-acolhe-workshop-de-apresentacao-do-projecto-de-plataforma-eletronica-de-trocas-de-documentos-comerciais-no-espaco-comunitario/) in April 2026.</details> | Advanced |
| Kenya | An [equity-trading service on the dominant wallet](https://www.telemediamagazine.com/vodacom-lesotho-brings-micro-investing-to-m-pesa-as-africas-mobile-wallets-move-into-wealth/) reportedly took 40 per cent of trades by number in its first days. <details><summary>Full record</summary>It launched in February 2026 with a brokerage, opening exchange equities to wallet customers without a conventional brokerage account, and accounted for about 5 per cent of daily trading value ([service](https://www.telemediamagazine.com/vodacom-lesotho-brings-micro-investing-to-m-pesa-as-africas-mobile-wallets-move-into-wealth/)).<br><br>A wallet becoming the largest retail route into the securities exchange by transaction count is the clearest instance in the base of payments infrastructure carrying a function it was not built for.</details> | Advanced |
| Lesotho | The [national e-invoicing platform is rolling out with business-to-business and business-to-government invoicing](https://snitechnology.net/lesotho-introduces-the-lekuka-national-e-invoicing-system/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2023-03-14 — [the revenue authority launched its e-payment and e-taxation platform](https://www.gov.ls/uncategorized/rsl-launches-e-payment-e-taxation-platforms/), the operative business-to-government rail.<br><br>2026-02-10 — [a payment gateway with a commercial bank went live for tax settlement](https://www.rsl.org.ls/sites/default/files/2026-02/Press%20Statement%20RSL%20SLB%20Integration%20E%20payments%20Launch%20%281%29.pdf).<br><br>2026-08-12 — [the national e-invoicing platform is rolling out nationwide, carrying business-to-business and business-to-government invoicing](https://snitechnology.net/lesotho-introduces-the-lekuka-national-e-invoicing-system/).<br><br>No transaction volume or value is published for any of the three.</details> | Advanced |
| Liberia | The state auditor found [US$36,768 paid in full](https://frontpageafricaonline.com/liberia-auditor-general-cites-missing-audit-committee-non-competitive-procurement-unverified-assets-delayed-projects-and-undelivered-digital-lending-platform/) for a digital lending platform that was contractually due live in May 2025 and never deployed. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The field verification was in May 2026 and the management letter issued in June ([audit](https://frontpageafricaonline.com/liberia-auditor-general-cites-missing-audit-committee-non-competitive-procurement-unverified-assets-delayed-projects-and-undelivered-digital-lending-platform/)).<br><br>The same audit found no functional project audit committee, a US$27,000 consultancy let without competitive procurement, and an agriculture commercialisation fund reporting dashboard never formally handed over to the ministry ([audit](https://frontpageafricaonline.com/liberia-auditor-general-cites-missing-audit-committee-non-competitive-procurement-unverified-assets-delayed-projects-and-undelivered-digital-lending-platform/)).</details> | Regressed |
| Libya | The Prime Minister [ordered treasuries to accept electronic payment of all state revenues](https://lgcc.gov.ly/%d8%b1%d8%a6%d9%8a%d8%b3-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%88%d8%b2%d8%b1%d8%a7%d8%a1-%d8%b9%d8%a8%d8%af%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%ad%d9%85%d9%8a%d8%af-%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%af%d8%a8%d9%8a%d8%a8%d8%a9-%d9%8a%d8%b5%d8%af%d8%b1/) in March 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The [decision](https://lgcc.gov.ly/%d8%b1%d8%a6%d9%8a%d8%b3-%d8%a7%d9%84%d9%88%d8%b2%d8%b1%d8%a7%d8%a1-%d8%b9%d8%a8%d8%af%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%ad%d9%85%d9%8a%d8%af-%d8%a7%d9%84%d8%af%d8%a8%d9%8a%d8%a8%d8%a9-%d9%8a%d8%b5%d8%af%d8%b1/) activates electronic collection of all public revenues and dues at Libyan treasuries, which is the mandate side.<br><br>The delivery side is smaller and specific: the central bank [exempted point-of-sale fees for tax, customs and port payments and deployed 130 terminals as a first phase](https://libyaherald.com/2026/05/cbl-source-to-libya-herald-cbl-moving-towards-ending-cash-collection-of-taxes-and-customs-duties-moving-solely-to-e-collections), moving toward ending cash collection. The bank's own [payment and settlement page](https://cbl.gov.ly/en/payment-and-settlement/) sets out the rails behind it and gives no business or government transaction figures.</details> | Advanced |
| Madagascar | A [digital-bank strategy](https://techcabal.com/2025/11/26/axian-rebrands-fintech-cluster/) serving about 500,000 merchants a month was announced in November 2025, alongside a [business transactions application](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/solutions/madagascar-orange-launches-mobile-app-for-business-transactions). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The fintech cluster was rebranded in November 2025 and its mobile money and payment products are being evolved toward a full digital bank across five markets, serving about 500,000 merchants a month ([strategy](https://techcabal.com/2025/11/26/axian-rebrands-fintech-cluster/)).<br><br>A competing operator launched a business transactions application in March 2025, still in service with no take-up figures published ([application](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/solutions/madagascar-orange-launches-mobile-app-for-business-transactions)). No government-to-business or business-to-government payment stream is separately recorded.</details> | Advanced |
| Malawi | [Electronic procurement became mandatory for all ministries and agencies from 1 April 2026](https://www.ppda.mw/newsdetail/58). <details><summary>Full record</summary>The business-to-government channel has run since 2018: the revenue authority's [electronic payment and payment-reference service settles tax through authorised dealer banks, on the same channel corporates already used for bank transfers](https://www.mra.mw/news/national-bank-of-malawi-dates-taxpayers-on-epayments).<br><br>2025-11 - the authority's annual report [records the customs platform upgraded with electronic payment integration for goods in transit, and continued rollout of the electronic invoicing system](https://www.mra.mw/admin/storage/attachment_files/1763367145_Annual%20Report%202024-25%20Edited%2015%20Nov%202025.pdf).<br><br>2026-03 - a government circular [made use of the national electronic procurement system mandatory for all ministries, departments and agencies from 1 April 2026, under section 71 of the procurement Act](https://www.ppda.mw/newsdetail/58). No transaction count, value or compliance figure has been published since the mandate took effect.</details> | Advanced |
| Mauritius | The [central switch](https://www.bom.mu/maucas-0) routes payments around the clock, and payment aggregators were brought under guideline in July 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The central automated switch is owned and operated by the central bank and routes payments among operators on a 24-hour basis ([switch](https://www.bom.mu/maucas-0)).<br><br>A guideline for payment aggregators was issued in July 2026, bringing them under a stated supervisory expectation for the first time ([guideline](https://www.bom.mu/sites/default/files/guideline_for_payment_aggregators_0.pdf)).<br><br>Business-to-business measures were carried in the 2026-2027 budget ([budget](https://mauritiusassembly.govmu.org/mauritiusassembly/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/SPEECH-2026_2027.pdf)). No transaction volume through the switch is published in the sources held.</details> | Advanced |
| Morocco | The bill settlement rail [opened to fintechs and invoicing software vendors by interface](https://www.cmi.co.ma/fr/nos-actualites/le-cmi-lance-fatourati-agregateur-et-ouvre-sa-plateforme-aux-fintechs-et-editeurs-de) in February 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The platform carried [218m transactions in 2024 and offered a plug-and-play collection add-on requiring no interface work](https://www.cmi.co.ma/fr/nos-actualites/fatourati-collect-le-cmi-ouvre-un-acces-simple-et-immediat-au-reglement-multicanal), which is the low end of the market.<br><br>In February 2026 the operator [opened the rail to fintechs and invoicing software vendors through an interface, on a cumulative 240m transactions and MAD 220bn since 2013](https://www.cmi.co.ma/fr/nos-actualites/le-cmi-lance-fatourati-agregateur-et-ouvre-sa-plateforme-aux-fintechs-et-editeurs-de). On the public side, suppliers to state enterprises [file invoices and track payment delays electronically](https://www.finances.gov.ma/fr/Pages/observatoire-delais-paiement.aspx).</details> | Advanced |
| Mozambique | The [instant payment system launched on 16 March 2026 with 24/7 availability, web, app and USSD access and free interbank transfers for individuals](https://www.bancomoc.mz/pt/media/noticias/governador-rogerio-zandamela-lanca-sistema-de-pagamentos-instantaneos-de-mocambique/), above a [treasury service linking the state system to banks and e-money institutions](https://www.mef.gov.mz/index.php/imprensa/discursos/2455-cge-2024-discurso-inicial-sexcia-mf-20-11-2025/file). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-03-16 — the central bank governor [launched the instant payment system, already legally created by an earlier notice, moving the rail from regulation into live operation: available 24 hours a day all year, funds available near-instantly, reachable by web, application and USSD, with interbank transfers free for individuals](https://www.bancomoc.mz/pt/media/noticias/governador-rogerio-zandamela-lanca-sistema-de-pagamentos-instantaneos-de-mocambique/).<br><br>The government-to-business side rests on an older service: the finance minister's address on the 2024 state accounts [describes the automated state payments service interlinking the treasury system with commercial banks and electronic money institutions to collect taxes, fees and other state charges electronically](https://www.mef.gov.mz/index.php/imprensa/discursos/2455-cge-2024-discurso-inicial-sexcia-mf-20-11-2025/file).<br><br>2025-09-03 — the ICT institute [met the state power utility on registering its planned online energy-payment application under the electronic services decree](https://intic.gov.mz/intic-e-edm-trabalham-para-modernizar-pagamento-de-energia-com-aplicativo-online/), a sectoral application moving toward regulatory compliance.<br><br>USSD access matters more here than the launch: it is what decides whether a rail reaches people without smartphones.</details> | Advanced |
| Namibia | [Open banking specifications](https://www.namibiansun.com/mw-main/namibia-moves-closer-to-instant-payments-NMH014446-3015-17893) are complete, with implementation named a priority under the payments vision and no date published. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The specifications were reported completed in July 2026 through the payments association's annual report, which is not itself held ([specifications](https://thebrief.com.na/2026/07/namibia-becomes-fourth-african-country-to-launch-instant-payments-system/)).<br><br>No participating institution, interface standard or pilot is named. Completed specifications with no implementation date are the same shape as the quick-response standard beside them.</details> | No change, specifications complete with no implementation date published |
| Nigeria | [Open banking](https://techcabal.com/2025/04/29/cbn-launches-open-banking/) was mandated from August 2025 across about 120m bank customers, a payments company gained [direct card-network access without a bank sponsor](https://businessday.ng/technology/article/teamapt-gains-direct-mastercard-network-access-in-strategic-payments-expansion/), and [embedded insurance](https://techcabal.com/2026/07/17/meet-the-team-behind-skydd/) opened to institutions. <details><summary>Full record</summary>Open banking was the first in Africa, mandating consented customer-data sharing across about 120m bank customers through identifier-tied consent, a central registry and standardised interfaces, four years after the framework ([open banking](https://techcabal.com/2025/04/29/cbn-launches-open-banking/)).<br><br>A Nigerian payments company gained direct access to a global card network as a non-bank acquirer, letting it onboard licensed merchants without a bank sponsor; no merchant count, pricing or settlement arrangement is stated ([access](https://businessday.ng/technology/article/teamapt-gains-direct-mastercard-network-access-in-strategic-payments-expansion/)).<br><br>Tier-one banks hold payments infrastructure inside the group rather than at arm's length, and the central bank has begun regulating fintech ownership as well as conduct ([ownership](https://techtrends.africa/the-rise-of-african-corporate-venture-capital/)); embedded insurance infrastructure opened to banks, fintechs, employers and merchants ([insurance](https://techcabal.com/2026/07/17/meet-the-team-behind-skydd/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Rwanda | A bank and mobile operator launched a [joint digital loan and savings product](https://taarifa.rw/2026/08/02/bpr-bank-rwanda-and-mtn-momo-rwanda-launch-mofaya-digital-loan-and-savings-solution) in August 2026, and the mobile money platform [migrated to a cloud-native vendor platform](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/21/ericsson-mtn-complete-momo-cloud-migration-across-four-african-markets/) across four markets. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The credit product launched on 2 August 2026, and the announcement names no regulator, consumer-protection arrangement or credit-data arrangement ([product](https://taarifa.rw/2026/08/02/bpr-bank-rwanda-and-mtn-momo-rwanda-launch-mofaya-digital-loan-and-savings-solution)).<br><br>The platform migration completed across four markets; the performance figures are the vendor's own and no downtime, cost or subscriber-impact figure is stated ([migration](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/21/ericsson-mtn-complete-momo-cloud-migration-across-four-african-markets/)).<br><br>A credit product built on operator data and a platform moved onto a foreign vendor's cloud in the same month is the private half of the payments picture, and neither carries a regulatory statement.</details> | Advanced |
| Seychelles | A [domestic fintech ran its first live transaction](https://techcabal.com/2026/07/22/fusepay-expands-beyond-payments-with-ai-operating-system/) on a virtual business-account platform, positioned against the cheque phase-out. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The first live transaction ran in July 2026 on a platform offering virtual business accounts, with the company also announcing an operating layer above it ([platform](https://techcabal.com/2026/07/22/fusepay-expands-beyond-payments-with-ai-operating-system/), [cheques](https://techafricanews.com/2025/11/21/seychelles-embraces-digital-revolution-as-fusepay-ushers-out-paper-cheques/), [funding](https://dabafinance.com/en/news/fusepay-rolls-out-b2b-payments-platform)).<br><br>A domestic company building business payments directly into the gap the central bank's cheque withdrawal creates is the clearest instance in this ledger of policy and private delivery lining up.<br><br>No transaction volume, customer count or pricing is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Somalia | The [national payment system](https://centralbank.gov.so/nps/) has carried bulk salary and vendor payment since July 2021, and a [reform strategy](https://mof.gov.so/sites/default/files/Publications/Somalia%20Public%20Financial%20Management%20Reform%20Strategy%20-%20March%2031.pdf) records paperless commitment-to-payment. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The central bank's own description sets out automated clearing, real-time gross settlement and interbank funds transfer in service since July 2021, with the clearing house supporting bulk salary and vendor-payment processing ([description](https://centralbank.gov.so/nps/)).<br><br>The reform strategy approved in March 2026 records daily bank reconciliation across all treasury single account balances and paperless commitment-to-payment processes as 2021 to 2024 achievements, and sets nationwide extension of the financial management system among twelve very-high-priority measures ([strategy](https://mof.gov.so/sites/default/files/Publications/Somalia%20Public%20Financial%20Management%20Reform%20Strategy%20-%20March%2031.pdf)).<br><br>A national single window for trade remains planned rather than operating.</details> | Advanced |
| South Africa | A rand-backed stablecoin was [listed on 7 August 2026](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/rand-goes-on-chain-as-zaru-hits-luno-global/xA9PO7NE6RAvo4J8) and [integrated into a merchant payments infrastructure twelve days later](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/19/ezeebit-integrates-zaru-to-expand-digital-asset-merchant-payments/), on the issuer's own account of use. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-07 — a rand-backed token was [listed on a global exchange for customers in four African countries](https://www.itweb.co.za/article/rand-goes-on-chain-as-zaru-hits-luno-global/xA9PO7NE6RAvo4J8), issued by an authorised financial services provider with a domestic asset manager holding the reserves and a monthly attestation.<br><br>2026-08-19 — a merchant payments provider [integrated the token into its infrastructure, letting its commerce partners make and receive payments in it](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/19/ezeebit-integrates-zaru-to-expand-digital-asset-merchant-payments/).<br><br>Two things the record makes plain and one it does not. The quoted pairs are against dollar tokens, so the on-chain liquidity of the rand token is denominated in someone else's currency; and the account of growing merchant use is the issuer's own, with no transaction count or value published. Whether any of it is business-to-business volume rather than listing activity, the base cannot say.</details> | Advanced |
| South Sudan | An [interbank payment and settlement system](https://boss.gov.ss/boss-launches-first-ever-electronic-interbank-payment-system/) launched with 13 banks and all licensed banks mandated by January 2026, and its [real-time settlement component](https://cajnewsafrica.com/2026/05/20/south-sudan-platform-to-digitise-economy-pays-off/) reported exceptional uptake. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The system launched in October 2025 with all licensed banks mandated onto it by 31 January 2026. It is wholesale settlement infrastructure rather than a retail instant rail, and the retail framing used at launch was promotional ([launch](https://boss.gov.ss/boss-launches-first-ever-electronic-interbank-payment-system/), [explainer](https://www.eyeradio.org/central-bank-explains-new-interbank-payment-system/)).<br><br>The settlement component's uptake was reported as exceptional by the central bank and the finance ministry, with no volume or value figure attached ([uptake](https://cajnewsafrica.com/2026/05/20/south-sudan-platform-to-digitise-economy-pays-off/)).<br><br>Wholesale settlement working while the retail rail has not started is the correct order, and the launch framing obscured it.</details> | Advanced |
| Sudan | The central bank [linked import and export transactions to the government platform](https://allafrica.com/stories/202512290216.html) in December 2025, and the first fully electronic export contract ran through it. <details><summary>Full record</summary>On 28 December 2025 the central bank inaugurated electronic integration of its import and export system with the government platform, linking foreign-trade transactions to national payment systems; the first fully electronic export contract was executed through it ([inauguration](https://allafrica.com/stories/202512290216.html)).<br><br>In July 2026 the standards body began taking certification fees through the same platform in coordination with the finance ministry ([integration](https://sudanhorizon.com/ssmo-integrates-eisali-payment-service-with-baladna-platform/)).<br><br>A wallet's new recurring-debit interface explicitly covers business collections and regulatory fees ([interface](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/zakhirwallet_zakher-digitalpayments-fintech-activity-7497610519567056897-U0ps)). None of the three carries a volume figure.</details> | Advanced |
| Tanzania | [All 47 participating providers](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026072316300213.pdf) are connected, [113 payment service providers are licensed](https://www.ippmedia.co.tz/the-guardian/news/local-news/read/interoperability-costs-cut-while-113-agencies-operate-payments-systems-2026-04-28-111407) with the central bank described as licensing aggressively to break monopolies, and [merchants accepting digital payments](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026060211494565.pdf) rose. <details><summary>Full record</summary>All 47 participants are connected on the annual report's count, with no earlier participant count held ([participants](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026072316300213.pdf)).<br><br>The finance minister told Parliament 113 providers were licensed, with the central bank described as licensing aggressively to break monopolies and no earlier count held ([licensed](https://www.ippmedia.co.tz/the-guardian/news/local-news/read/interoperability-costs-cut-while-113-agencies-operate-payments-systems-2026-04-28-111407)).<br><br>Merchant acceptance comes from the central bank's national payment systems annual report for 2025 ([merchants](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026060211494565.pdf), [cashless](https://dailynews.co.tz/cashless-shift-poised-to-transform-economy/)), and a credit reference data system was developed and deployed with onboarding under way — extended by regulation rather than by architecture, and its data subjects are borrowers ([credit](https://www.bot.go.tz/Publications/Regular/Annual%20Report/en/2026072316300213.pdf)).</details> | Advanced |
| Uganda | A [wallet insurance distribution product](https://techafricanews.com/2026/07/22/airtel-money-uganda-and-britam-partner-to-expand-digital-insurance-and-financial-services/) went live, a [tokenised-asset exchange](https://techcabal.com/2026/04/08/global-settlement-holdings-to-acquire-majority-stake-in-akiba/) changed hands, and a [bank and platform memorandum](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/business/centenary-micropay-partner-to-expand-digital-NV_237612_072026) carries a credit-scoring layer with nothing on file about consent, retention or legal basis. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The insurance product's first offering went live in July 2026 with no premium, policy-count or target published ([insurance](https://techafricanews.com/2026/07/22/airtel-money-uganda-and-britam-partner-to-expand-digital-insurance-and-financial-services/)).<br><br>A majority stake in the tokenised-asset exchange moved in April 2026 ([exchange](https://techcabal.com/2026/04/08/global-settlement-holdings-to-acquire-majority-stake-in-akiba/)).<br><br>The bank and platform memorandum's signature date is unestablished, and nothing is on file about consent, retention or legal basis for the credit-scoring layer ([memorandum](https://www.newvision.co.ug/category/business/centenary-micropay-partner-to-expand-digital-NV_237612_072026)). A short-code micro-investing pilot reaches the base only as a comparator in a foreign-led survey, with no domestic source, provider name or launch date held ([pilot](https://www.telemediamagazine.com/vodacom-lesotho-brings-micro-investing-to-m-pesa-as-africas-mobile-wallets-move-into-wealth/)).</details> | Advanced |
| Zambia | A [2025 Act mandates an electronic single window and single payment point for trade duties](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/documents/acts/Act%20No.%208%20of%202025%20-%20The%20Border%20Management%20and%20Trade%20Facilitation%20Act%20.pdf); a [new payment Act is passed but not commenced](https://zambialii.org/akn/zm/act/2026/5/eng@2026-04-08). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-08 — [the border management and trade facilitation statute mandates an electronic single window and a single payment point platform for import, export and transit duties, taxes, fees and levies](https://www.parliament.gov.zm/sites/default/files/documents/acts/Act%20No.%208%20of%202025%20-%20The%20Border%20Management%20and%20Trade%20Facilitation%20Act%20.pdf) — a direct business-to-government payment integration for traders.<br><br>2026-04-08 — [a new National Payment System Act replaces the 2007 statute, creating licensing and market-conduct rules for payment service providers covering these rails](https://zambialii.org/akn/zm/act/2026/5/eng@2026-04-08). It is not yet commenced, which is the operative fact: the regime that would govern these payments is enacted and not in force.<br><br>No transaction volume or value on either rail is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Zimbabwe | A [digital capital-market venue](https://positiveeyenews.co.zw/2026/07/31/zse-and-lloyd-corporate-capital-partner-to-unlock-growth-capital-for-zimbabwes-smes-through-zeex/) was described as newly approved at the signing of a growth-capital memorandum, with no approval date for the venue itself stated. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The memorandum is an intent instrument and no approval date for the venue is stated ([venue](https://positiveeyenews.co.zw/2026/07/31/zse-and-lloyd-corporate-capital-partner-to-unlock-growth-capital-for-zimbabwes-smes-through-zeex/)).<br><br>A capital-market venue described as approved, at an event about something else, with no approval date, is the whole of the record.</details> | Advanced |

## P2P, P2G and P2B functionality

| Country | Developments | Progress |
|---|---|---|
| Algeria | The [domestic instant payment system announced in October 2024 has no launch date held](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/algeria-to-launch-instant-payment-system-for-faster-more-efficient-transactions), and a QR wallet is unconfirmed. <details><summary>Full record</summary>The instant payment system was [announced in October 2024 to replace settlement of up to seventy-two hours, and no launch date is held as at 2026-08-03](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/algeria-to-launch-instant-payment-system-for-faster-more-efficient-transactions).<br><br>A [mobile QR payment service was announced for a January 2025 launch by a state bank, an Islamic bank and the postal operator, and nothing held confirms it went live](https://al24news.dz/en/algeria-set-to-launch-mobile-payment-system-dz-mob-pay-in-january-2025); the source is year-precision.<br><br>So the country's card estate grew by millions of cards and hundreds of billions of dinars in the window while both of its account-to-account retail rails stayed announcements.</details> | No change |
| Angola | The instant payment scheme [is wallet-led, with the largest wallet at 52.2% of transfers and 66.4% of transfers under Kz 10,000](https://angop.ao/noticias/economia/paypay-ultrapassa-1-6-milhoes-de-transferencias-mensais-no-kwik/). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-03-05 — the monthly operator report [puts the scheme as wallet-led and concentrated: the largest wallet at 52.2% of transfers, more than 12.3m registered accounts, about 24.8m active payment keys, and 66.4% of transfers under Kz 10,000](https://angop.ao/noticias/economia/paypay-ultrapassa-1-6-milhoes-de-transferencias-mensais-no-kwik/), against [35m transfers and Kz 590bn over 2025 across 22 banking and non-banking institutions](https://angop.ao/noticias/economia/operacoes-no-kwik-registam-crescimento-acima-dos-100/).<br><br>The small-value share is the finding: this is retail person-to-person traffic, and the concentration in one wallet is a single point of failure the record does not discuss. The base holds no launch date and no figure dated before 2025, so the growth rate cited by the operator cannot be checked against an independent series.<br><br>Nothing on the ledger covers business-to-business or business-to-government payment functionality except through the tax authority's invoicing mandate, reported under revenue collection.</details> | Advanced |
| Benin | The regional instant payment rail [launched across all eight member states in September 2025, free domestically](https://lanation.bj/economie/lancement-du-systeme-de-paiement-instantane-interoperable-la-pi-spi-une-solution-rapide-et-inclusive-au-profit-des-citoyens-uemoa), with banks and mobile money fully interconnected by May 2026. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2025-09-30 — [the regional instant payment rail launched across all eight member states, free for domestic transfers, with banks and mobile money fully interconnected by May 2026](https://lanation.bj/economie/lancement-du-systeme-de-paiement-instantane-interoperable-la-pi-spi-une-solution-rapide-et-inclusive-au-profit-des-citoyens-uemoa), against a rail that had only just launched at the start of the window.<br><br>Free and fully interconnected is as complete as any payment position on this ledger gets. What is not published is use: no transaction count, value or Benin-specific participation figure appears, so the rail's reach is asserted by its architecture rather than measured.<br><br>The domestic layer riding on it is a home-grown aggregator, reported under national production capacity.</details> | Advanced |
| Botswana | The [switch’s second phase is intended to connect banks and mobile-money providers](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/Ministry%20of%20FInance%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%2026-02-2026%20-%20Final.pdf), and the [regulatory sandbox opened in March 2026](https://www.bankofbotswana.bw/sites/default/files/press-release-files/Public%20Notice%20-%20Call%20for%20Applications%20for%20entry%20into%20the%20Bank%20of%20Botswana%20Regulatory%20Sandbox.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>2022-02 — [the central bank’s licensing guidelines define and license the electronic payment categories, person-to-person and person-to-business among them](https://bankofbotswana.bw/sites/default/files/Electronic%20Payment%20Services%20Guidelines%20February%202022_1.pdf).<br><br>2026-02-26 — [the second phase of the retail payment switch is intended to connect banks and mobile-money providers](https://www.botswanaspeaks.gov.bw/media/COMMITTEE%20OF%20SUPPPLY%20SPEECHES/Ministry%20of%20FInance%20Committee%20of%20Supply%20Speech%2026-02-2026%20-%20Final.pdf).<br><br>2026-03-04 — [the central bank opened its regulatory sandbox to applicants on a theme of inclusive payments and interoperability](https://www.bankofbotswana.bw/sites/default/files/press-release-files/Public%20Notice%20-%20Call%20for%20Applications%20for%20entry%20into%20the%20Bank%20of%20Botswana%20Regulatory%20Sandbox.pdf).<br><br>No retail transaction volume or value is published.</details> | Advanced |
| Burkina Faso | The regional instant-payment rail [is live with nine domestic institutions authorised](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article144889), and [connection deadlines were pushed to September 2026 and June 2027](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article147670). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The rail was in a real-condition test phase with a union launch announced for September 2025](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article139887) and [became operational at the union launch](https://www.sidwaya.info/espace-uemoa-le-systeme-de-paiement-instantane-pi-spi-de-la-bceao-operationnel/). 2026-03-08 — [nine domestic institutions were authorised by February 2026](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article144889), with 80 institutions connected union-wide by June 2026.<br><br>2026-07-07 — [connection deadlines were pushed to September 2026 for banks and payment institutions and June 2027 for microfinance](https://lefaso.net/spip.php?article147670). The extension is a union-wide decision rather than a domestic one, so the slippage is not the country's alone; it is still slippage against a rail that domestic uptake depends on.<br><br>No domestic transaction count or value on the rail is published.</details> | Advanced, with connection deadlines slipped |
| Burundi | The national instant payment system [launched in April 2026 and had 17 of 78 licensed institutions live by August](https://www.jimberemag.org/une-revolution-de-leconomie-digitale-au-burundi/), with the four mobile-money systems interoperable. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-04-23 — [the national instant payment platform launched publicly, connecting banks, microfinance institutions and mobile money on the international messaging standard and USSD at around 1,000 transactions per second](https://www.burunditimes.com/new-burundipay-platform-connects-banks-microfinance-and-mobile-money-networks/), [reported as Africa's twenty-second live instant payment system](https://www.wearetech.africa/en/fils-uk/news/tech/burundi-launches-real-time-payments-platform-backed-by-world-bank).<br><br>2026-08-06 — [17 of 78 licensed institutions were in production by 4 August 2026 — five commercial banks, ten microfinance institutions and two payment institutions — with about 224,000 users and cumulative transaction volume of about BIF 4.29bn, and integration mandatory for all licensed institutions by 5 September 2026 under a penalty of BIF 1m per month of delay](https://www.jimberemag.org/une-revolution-de-leconomie-digitale-au-burundi/).<br><br>2026-06-12 — [the four mobile-money systems are named as interoperable through it](https://burundi-eco.com/le-gouvernement-accelere-t-il-la-digitalisation-des-services-publics/), where they had not been. Fewer than a quarter of licensed institutions are live four months after launch, which is what the September deadline and its penalty exist to change.</details> | Advanced |
| Cameroon | An [interoperable QR-code standard launched at Douala in July 2026](https://leconomie.info/article/paiement-numerique-le-gimac-officialise-la-norme-qr-code-interoperable-de-la-cemac) lets any wallet or bank application scan any merchant code. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-07-29 - the standard [went live so that any bank, microfinance or mobile-money application can scan any merchant code across the six member states, routed and settled through the regional switch](https://leconomie.info/article/paiement-numerique-le-gimac-officialise-la-norme-qr-code-interoperable-de-la-cemac). No count of merchants or applications on it has been published since.<br><br>The state operator's own wallet, [launched on 1 May 2025, has never published a subscriber, wallet or transaction figure](https://lejour.cm/mobile-money-le-lancement-de-blue-money-annonce/) and [was still without one in December 2025](https://lejour.cm/camtel-ethio-telecom-alliance-strategique-pour-lessor-de-blue-money/), so the one nationally owned retail payment instrument on this ledger cannot be assessed at all.<br><br>2026-08-31 — a domestic technology company [launched a payments platform carrying transfers across mobile-money networks, wallet management, bill and airtime payment, payment links and developer interfaces](https://techafricanews.com/2026/08/31/looping-binary-launches-lbpay-digital-payments-cameroon/). It is a launch announcement on the company's own account: no user, merchant or transaction figure, licence or partner bank is named.</details> | Advanced |
| Cape Verde | [Rede vinti4 is the national retail switch, and cards were the most-used payment instrument in 2024](https://www.bcv.cv/SiteCollectionDocuments/2025/Relat%C3%B3rio%20de%20Sistema%20de%20Pagamentos%202024.pdf). <details><summary>Full record</summary>[The Banco de Cabo Verde's payment-systems report for 2024, reported on 24 September 2025, makes the card the most-used payment instrument in the country, at a daily average of 132,405 card operations worth CVE 553.8m](https://www.bcv.cv/SiteCollectionDocuments/2025/Relat%C3%B3rio%20de%20Sistema%20de%20Pagamentos%202024.pdf). [Over the 2025 Christmas period Cabo Verdeans moved more than CVE 9bn through the vinti4 network](https://www.inforpress.cv/article-6873).<br><br>The base holds transaction volumes and no breakdown by instrument type, merchant category or island, and no acceptance-point count.</details> | No change, a standing system the base holds for the first time |
| Central African Republic | State electricity bills [became payable over mobile money at an official launch in August 2026](https://oubanguimedias.com/2026/08/07/centrafrique-telecel-et-enerca-signent-un-partenariat-pour-digitaliser-le-paiement-des-factures-delectricite/), on a contract dated October 2025. <details><summary>Full record</summary>2026-08-04 — [state electricity bills became officially payable over one operator's mobile-money service, for subscribers including those in five named towns, under a contract dated October 2025](https://oubanguimedias.com/2026/08/07/centrafrique-telecel-et-enerca-signent-un-partenariat-pour-digitaliser-le-paiement-des-factures-delectricite/). No tariff, transaction fee, volume, revenue share or exclusivity term is disclosed, the two companies are the only announcers of record and no regulator is named.<br><br>The report states subscribers were already paying this way before the ceremony, so the launch date belongs to the agreement rather than to first use.<br><br>It is the first person-to-government payment channel on this ledger and it is a bilateral arrangement between a utility and one operator, in a country where a single operator's month-long outage has already stopped salary payments.</details> | Advanced |
